# Decisions

A dated log of product and architecture decisions, newest first. Each says what was decided and why. A newer decision can replace an older one: add a new entry that says so, and leave the old one in place.

To propose a change to a decision, open an issue with the **Proposal** template or a draft pull request. The owner decides.

---

## 2026-11-25

### Locking travels, opening stays on the device
This replaces "Section locks stay on each device" (below), keeping its rule that a lock opened on one device never opens sections on another.
- **Decided (owner):** locking a category on any of the person's devices locks it on all of them, a new one included; opening it with Face ID opens it only on the device in the person's hand. *Why:* a new iPad opened every category locked on the iPhone without Face ID, listed them in its search and named them in its reminders. *Alternative:* full sync, where opening on one device opens everywhere (a less-guarded device could open the phone's sections).
- **Decided:** database version 19 adds `CategoryTemplate.lockedEverywhereAt`, when the category was last locked anywhere, written when a lock goes on (never cleared, not in backups, which carry the device's locks as before). Each device keeps when it made each choice beside its choices (`SectionLocks`, a `.times` file); a category is locked when it was locked anywhere after this device last chose for it, else as chosen here, else as the catalog says. A choice whose time isn't known reads as older, so locked (locks fail closed). Each device sends up the locks it had once, at launch (`LockPublishing`): a device that had opened one of them is locked again there once, until opened with Face ID.

### „Înlocuiește tot” on a binder that syncs
- **Decided (owner):** replacing everything with a backup stays, made safe: a copy of the binder is kept first (`KeptCopies`, „Copia dosarului de dinainte de „Înlocuiește tot””, in Setări › iCloud și copii de siguranță), the warning says it deletes on every device when the binder syncs, and it waits while the binder may still be arriving from iCloud. *Why:* it deletes on every device, and on a device still downloading it would replace only half. *Alternatives:* hide it while sync is on (no way to roll the whole binder back); leave it.

### Two devices changing one section at once
- **Decided (owner):** 1.0 keeps a section's values, its rows under Date importante, a deadline's reminders and a category's definition as one stored field each; two devices changing different values of one section at the same time keep only one change. Storing them value by value comes after 1.0. *Why:* it is rare, and it is a large change to the database.

## 2026-11-24

### A deletion from another device never takes what this one added
- **Decided (owner):** no relationship deletes what it points at; the app deletes what a record holds itself (`RecordContents`), and each held record keeps its holder's id (database version 18, `ParentLinks`). A record another device's deletion let go of goes back to a copy of its holder with that id (two copies joined elsewhere), or is offered in *De corectat* to be kept in a section of its own, „Recuperate” (`LostRecordsStep`). *Why:* iCloud brings deletions in as they are, so with `.cascade` a device that added a deadline or a document offline to a section the other device deleted, or joined with its copy, lost it without a word. *Alternatives:* the same without the ids (a joined copy's records would only wait in *De corectat*), or leaving it for after 1.0. An undo receipt now reads what a deleted record holds from the same list the app deletes by (`RecordContents.owned`), no longer from the schema's cascade rules ("a delete comes back whole", below).

### iCloud sync in 1.0
- **Decided (owner):** version 1.0 ships with iCloud sync on by default, so the binder is on the person's iPhone and iPad and comes back on a new phone by itself. *Why:* an App Store version that keeps the binder only on the phone (and in the iPhone's own backup or a backup file the person remembers to make) isn't one the owner wants to publish. *Alternative:* 1.0 without sync, and sync in a later version; it was the plan until now, kept while the data layout settled.
- **Decided:** before each deployment of the layout to production, a development build writes the whole layout to CloudKit's development environment (`CloudKitSchemaSetup`, the *DosarCuSina (iCloud layout)* scheme). *Why:* iCloud learns fields only from records a build uploads, so fields no development build has written yet would be missing in production, and phones writing them would fail to sync. From the first deployment on, a stored field is only ever added.
- **Decided:** no TestFlight build before the layout is in production, then a TestFlight round on two devices before the App Store submission.

## 2026-11-18

### The App Store listing: Romanian only, light captioned screenshots, a drawn household
- **Decided (owner):** the App Store listing is in Romanian only, the app's one language; no English localization of the listing (`marketing/app-store/ro.md`). *Alternative:* an English listing saying the app is in Romanian, for phones set to English; not now.
- **Decided (owner):** the screenshots are framed in one style: a light background, the caption above in SF Pro, the device in a thin bezel running off the bottom (`marketing/frame-screenshots.swift`). The two other drafts (the icon's night blue with the whole device; the app's blue without a bezel) are gone. Only light captures are framed; the dark ones stay as raw captures.
- **Decided:** the order is Dosar, Urmează, the car, reading an invoice, the trip, search, the passport, the same for the iPhone 6.9″ and the iPad 13″, taken by the UI tests (`DosarCuSinaScreenshots`) and never by hand. The reading scene shows where the practice invoice lands for the household's car („Am fost la service”, its rows ticked), in the household's names (Service Auto Nord SRL, Ana Dosarescu); the practice invoice itself is unchanged.
- **Decided (owner):** the household's pictures are drawn in code, nothing downloaded or generated elsewhere, in development builds only (`ScreenshotPictures`): PawPaw, a grey tabby with green eyes, a white belly and large paws, and a passport's data page marked „SPECIMEN” across it, with made-up values only (a CNP of county 99 with its control digit, a made-up number and its machine-readable lines). The passport screen stays only while its drawing looks right; otherwise it is dropped.

### Sheets open page-sized, square boxes on wide screens, a document's page lists its values once, labour is no part (App Store screenshots)
- **Decided:** every sheet, the shell's and a screen's own, takes its size from one place, `dsSheetSizing()`: a page (`presentationSizing(.page)`) where there is room around it, so on an iPad a form opens tall enough to show its rows and its pinned action. *Alternative:* the form size (`.form`), Apple's usual for a short form; smaller on a large iPad, and some of ours (the visit form, the capture review) are long. On an iPhone nothing changes.
- **Decided:** on a document's page its category's values are the page's own list („Date de pe act”, the secrets in their card); Date importante there keeps only the rows the person added (`DocumentPageFacts.listedAbove`, `keyFactRows`, which the pocket and the „Adaugă…” menu both read). *Why:* a passport's „Serie și număr” showed twice.
- **Decided:** a line item whose words are labour (`CaptureLexicon.labourWords`: „manoperă”, „mână de lucru”, hours worked; each word only where a word starts, never „labor”, which „laborator” starts with) is kept with no part and its cost as labour, wherever a line item is written (`HouseholdMutations.addItems`), and a visit's title counts only the work (`LabourLine.work`). Visits saved before are offered in *De corectat* (owner: „Yes, offer the repair”): `LabourPartsStep`, a correction at the end of the registry, finds each line kept as a part of one's own whose words are labour and, once the person approves, keeps it as the visit's labour (no part, its cost the labour's, the visit's cost unchanged), in the repairs' one app-made unit, idempotently.
- **Decided (owner: „Square boxes”):** on a regular width the binder's boxes are square, sized by the width: three a row when the grid's room is taller than wide, four when wider (`DSBoxGrid.plan`, the one rule, by the room and never a device's model or size; `dsAtLeastSquare` lets a box grow taller when its words need it). A compact width keeps two boxes a row, as tall as their words, and accessibility sizes one. *Alternatives:* taller boxes on wide screens (a binder with few boxes still leaves the screen empty), the binder beside Urmează's next deadlines (repeats a tab, the most design work).
- **Decided (owner: „Beside the date”):** whether a value's buttons fit beside it is decided on its title and value only; what is said under it (a passport's travel line, how urgent its date is, a secret's notes) sits under the row at its full width and wraps there (`DSValueWithActions`'s `below`), for every value row.
- **Decided:** a search result's preview line is the best matching field that says something the row's title and subtitle don't (`SearchIndex.snippet`), for every kind of result; none when everything that matched is already said.
- **Decided:** a currency is named in one place, `Money.currencyName`: the leu „lei”, as amounts are written, any other by its code; every money field's menu reads it.

### What capture learns stays the issuer's, behind the binder's locks, and never outranks the kind's own labels
- **Decided (replaces "the issuer's label wins where they read the field another way" of „Capture learns where an issuer prints a value, by the page's positions”):** a learned label fills what the kind's own table misses and never overrides what it reads. A lesson under a label outside the kind's table is read after the kind's readers and counts only where they read the field at none of the kind's own labels for it (they read nothing, or guessed, such as the first later date for the day to pay by): `ReadingArbiter.admitsLearned`, the one rule, by whether the kind's reading's own line holds one of the kind's wordings of the field. A lesson whose label is one of the kind's own wordings of the field (a column the kind's readers read on rows joined across the page) is that label read where the issuer prints it, and is weighed with their readings as before. *Why this one and not weighing every learned reading below the kind's:* the same effect where they disagree on a labelled value, but a guess of the kind's readers would then always beat what the issuer's own label says, and the first lesson capture ever learned (a due date the phone took from the next reading's day) would never be read. *Why:* a short label learned of one paper could otherwise beat „Total de plată” on every later bill of that firm.
- **Decided:** on a page with positions, a value's label is the nearest line above it in its own column or the nearest line to its left on its row with nothing between them, whichever reads as a label; when both do, none: on a two-column bill the text to its left may be the other column's, and which is its own can't be told. *Alternative:* the column's label always first; it would learn a row's earlier value („Ordin de plată” above an amount whose row label is „Total de plată”) as a label. *Left as it is:* text to its left in another column, with nothing above the value in its own, is still taken as its label; told to the readers, it must still read the value again on that page.
- **Decided:** a label holding a word of what the binder keeps that names the household is never a lesson, wherever in the binder it is kept, not only on the paper being saved (`HouseholdValues`: the names of the sections that play a person, and every value of an address, plate or email field, by the field's type and the category's part, never a built-in's key), nor one holding a proper name the page prints in its buyer's block (`RomanianFieldExtractor.buyerBlock`, the one answer `issuerName` reads too: a word that starts with a capital and is no word of a label table). Lessons kept before this rule that hold one are offered to be forgotten (`PersonalLessonLabelsStep`, in *De corectat*), the issuer unnamed when its lessons are protected. *Why:* lessons sync and travel in backups, and must hold only an issuer's words.
- **Decided:** each lesson keeps the category of the section its paper was filed in (`IssuerLesson.categoryKey`, database version 17, a lightweight stage; version 16 frozen; in backups as `categoryKey`, missing when not known). Settings › *Ce am învățat* shows an issuer all of whose lessons come from locked categories as „Firmă dintr-o categorie protejată”, without its name or labels, until Face ID opens the binder (`IssuerLessons.isProtected`, through `BinderLocks`, the one lock answer); a lesson whose category isn't known (learned before version 17) counts as protected while any lock is on, as locks fail closed. *Alternative:* the category from the paper's kind; a kind can be filed in more than one category, and the person chooses where.
- **Decided:** what a paper teaches is worked out away from the main thread before the filing's unit opens (`CaptureFiler.lessons(taughtBy:household:)`, a detached task), with the same result as before; the unit takes only quick synchronous work.
- **Decided:** a PDF page is read by text recognition when its text covers only a little of what its pictures show: pictures over a quarter of the page or more, and text lines under a twenty-fifth of what they cover (`PDFTextCoverage`; `PDFPagePictures` measures the images the page's content draws, forms included). *Why:* a scanner app's footer made a scanned page look digital, and nothing on it was read. *Alternative:* telling by the rendered page's ink outside the text; shaded headers and rules of digital invoices would read as a scan.

### Words that agree, one word for a deadline, and layouts that give way (break-it of 18 November)
- **Decided (follows #109):** capture, Adaugă and onboarding say „document” of any paper („Verifică documentul”, „Fotografiază un document”, „Document protejat” in De verificat); „act” stays for a personal document's own page and flow. Checked by `act-for-any-paper` (Views/Capture, Views/Add, Views/Onboarding). A row in place of something behind Face ID that is a document, whatever it is (a person's identity card, something shared), is „Document protejat” too, from its one source (`LockedWording.document`, #123). *Alternative:* the kind's noun in the review's title („Verifică factura”); not done, since the title shows before and while the kind changes.
- **Decided:** a deadline is a „termen” everywhere the app speaks, Siri, Shortcuts, the widget and notifications included („Termen protejat”, „Ai un termen mâine”, „Ce termene am”, a reminder's body „… · până pe 14 noiembrie”); `one-word-for-a-deadline` refuses „obligație” and „scadență” outside capture's tables. Siri's answer is „Gata, l-am bifat.”
- **Decided:** what a repeat counts from is said of no noun: „De la data rezolvării”, „Următorul e la 3 zile după rezolvare.”, „… după termen, nu după rezolvare.” *Why not from the deadline's noun and gender, as asked:* a deadline declares no gender (one of one's own never can, and the copy guide says only what declares one takes one), so a gendered form would need a default; the noun-free wording fits every deadline, a rovinietă bought as well as an ITP done.
- **Decided:** a paper's last valid day is one choice, `PocketWording.validity` and `validityLabel`: „Expiră pe …” or „A expirat pe …”, in Acte, a person's documents, a paper's page and a document's hero; never „Valabil până la”. Form fields keep their label „Valabil până la” (an editable value, not a statement); changing a built-in field's label is left for a catalog change of its own.
- **Decided:** a visit's paper read without rows says what it filled in, only that („Am trecut data și suma de pe ea.”), and when nothing: „Nu am găsit pe ea data, suma sau numele. Completează de mână.” (`VisitDraft.apply` returns the facts, `VisitForm.readNote(found:filled:)`).
- **Decided:** the permission prompts speak as the app („Cer Face ID ca să deschid…”, „Ca să fotografiezi facturi… pe care le păstrez în dosar.”); `ReleaseReadinessTests` refuses „noi” and „Aplicația”. Every built-in is offered as one thing („Bicicletă”, „Plantă”, „Espressor”, „Fișă medicală”, „Alt obiect”, „Chiriaș”; catalog 27); the gas service is „Gaze”; the iPhone's settings are „Configurări”.
- **Decided:** one readable width for every screen (`DS.ReadableWidth`, `dsReadableWidth()`, 672 pt at the standard text size and growing with it; `.grid`, 1024 pt, for the binder's boxes), on every vertical `ScrollView` of a screen and on the pinned bar; `screen-without-readable-width` and `readable-width-by-hand` check it. The share sheet keeps its own narrower width. *Why:* the earlier 640 and 720 pt were two widths for one job, and nine screens had none.
- **Decided:** a refused save is said once, for every form: `DSValidationReveal` with `dsRevealsValidation(_:)` shows every problem, scrolls to the highest, moves VoiceOver there and says it, at every refusal (`DSFieldContainer` takes part by itself, a form's own line through `dsValidationTarget`).
- **Decided:** the plain undo toast keeps „Anulează” beside its words while the button takes no more of the width than the words keep, else under them (`WordsBesideAction`, measured, never by text size). *Alternative:* `DSAdaptiveStack`; not used, since it would move the button under every long message at the standard size too.
- **Decided:** icon wells, thumbnails and the onboarding's drawing grow with the text up to the largest standard size (`DS.Control.largestDecorationSize`), so the words keep the row; `DSCompactButton` wraps only when it doesn't fit; `DSLinkButton` is a text action whose label is its 44 pt (`hit-target-outside-button`); „Altă zi” steps like the repeat (`DSStepsStepper` over `RepeatChoice.stepped`); the widget's tone text uses the app's text colours (moved to `ios/Shared/StatusColors.xcassets`) and its rows grow, showing fewer when they don't fit.
- **Decided (owner, answers the break-it's „Înlocuit”):** a document replaced through „Am rezolvat” keeps the badge „Expirat” (`DSStatusBadge.replaced`), whatever its printed date. *Why, in the owner's words:* the moment a new passport or identity card is issued the old one is no longer valid (it is physically cancelled), so „Expirat” is true. Not to be reopened by later reviews.
### A person can have several citizenships, and travels on the most favourable (owner)
- **Decided (owner):** a person keeps every citizenship their documents show, as a list on them (*Cetățenie*, a multiple choice, `MemberFields.citizenship`), and adds or takes one away on their page. Filing a passport, identity card or residence permit adds the one it prints; only the person's page takes one away. *Why:* a dual citizen (Romanian and Moldovan, say) whose Moldovan passport was filed first kept only Moldova, for good, and the trip check then treated them as a citizen of a country outside the EU and told them they couldn't come back into the country without a residence permit.
- **Decided (owner):** the trip check goes by the most favourable citizenship for each trip, with the document that goes with it: each citizenship's rules are checked with the documents that stand for it (those printing it, or no nationality), and the one that leaves the least to fix wins, then the one that asks for less, then a free-movement one. A Romanian and Moldovan citizen goes into the EU on the Romanian identity card or passport; a Moldovan passport is never a Romanian citizen's document.
- **Decided:** the single value an earlier version kept becomes a one-item list by itself (`ChoiceKeysStep`, which already turns a value kept as text into its field's form: only how it is stored changes, so no schema version). A second citizenship a person's documents print that the earlier version left out is a correction in *De corectat* (`CitizenshipsStep`), asked first, since it changes what the person's page says.
- **Decided:** a long multiple choice shows the chosen options as chips, each taken away with a tap, and *Adaugă* opens the same searchable list a long choice opens (`DSMultiChoiceField`, beyond `DSChoiceField.menuLimit`).

### Where a trip goes, as the rules tell places apart
- **Decided:** three regions instead of two. *În UE sau în spațiul Schengen* names Switzerland, Norway, Iceland and Liechtenstein, where an EU citizen travels with an identity card (they were counted „În afara UE” before). *În Moldova sau în Balcanii de Vest* (Moldova, Serbia, Montenegro, North Macedonia, Albania, Bosnia and Herzegovina): Romania's Ministry of Foreign Affairs travel pages (read 18 November 2026) say each lets a Romanian citizen in with a valid identity card or passport, kept as data with its source (`TravelRules.identityCards`), with the document valid three months after the return, the longest any of them asks (North Macedonia, Bosnia and Herzegovina). Other EU citizens there are asked for a passport until their own rules are checked; Kosovo isn't in the region (the ministry's pages send travellers there with a passport). *În altă țară* is the rest. The stored raw values are kept, so trips already planned read the same.
- **Decided:** an identity card without a chip counts for travel until 2 August 2031 at the latest (Regulation (EU) 2019/1157, art. 5: such cards cease to be valid by 3 August 2031), and the simple identity card issued since 2025 (*Simplă (CIS)*, a new variant) is no travel document (the Ministry of Internal Affairs). The older card's variant is now named *Fără cip, modelul vechi*. *Left open for the owner:* the regulation keeps cards of people who were 70 or over on 2 August 2021 valid until they expire; the check doesn't know a holder's age and applies the 2031 day to everyone.
- **Decided:** a family member's residence card (*Carte de rezidență*) and a British citizen's residence document under the Withdrawal Agreement are variants of the residence permit, so they meet the same need and are read by its template. *Why variants, not types:* they are the same right in the same uniform format, and a type of their own would need a second reader for the same card.
- **Decided:** a day already passed is said as passed everywhere in the trip check („A expirat pe 1 mai 2026.”), and a rabies booster given before the one before ran out counts at once, however close to the trip (Regulation (EU) No 576/2013, Annex III); one after a lapse waits its 21 days, and when the binder can't tell, the person is asked to look in the pet's passport.

### Public pages say what is true now
- **Decided:** the help page says the binder moves to a new phone with a backup, and that iCloud sync comes in a later version, with no date; the privacy policy says the App Store version doesn't sync yet. Both languages, dated 18 November 2026.
- **Decided:** before a document goes to a compatible AI service, which has no terms to link, the review says its rules are its own and to read them (the words Settings already used, now one source, `AIKeyWording.ownServiceRules`); the privacy policy says which providers are linked and where.
- **Decided (owner):** the help page, in both languages, gives a support e-mail address as a contact that needs no account, beside GitHub: the owner's chosen public address, kept once in `PublicPages.supportEmail` (`PublicPagesTests` checks the pages agree), with the same warning to send no photos of documents or personal details.
- **Decided:** plain `http` to a home server (`NSAllowsLocalNetworking`) with the local network's words (`NSLocalNetworkUsageDescription`), checked by `ReleaseReadinessTests` and `scripts/bundle-check.sh`; the privacy policy lists the local network among what the app asks for.

### Onboarding and full pages
- **Decided:** onboarding offers examples only while nothing of the person's is in the binder: once its photo step filed a document, the last step opens the binder instead (`OnboardingFlow.start`).
- **Decided:** a screenshot's full page is withheld only while something is locked right now: the app lock, or a screen showing a locked state. The app-switcher cover, up after any Face ID whenever the app isn't active (the screenshot editor's moment included), hides the app from the app switcher and no longer withholds it (`FullPageScreenshot.withholds`).
### Reminders and what acts from outside the app, at their causes
- **Decided:** a reminder's day and time reach iOS in the app's calendar. Its date parts carry `Calendar.dosar` (Gregorian), made in one place (`ReminderTrigger`), and no time zone, so it fires on that day at that hour wherever the phone is, whatever calendar the phone is set to. A new architecture check (`trigger-outside-reminder-trigger`) keeps every trigger there. *Why:* date parts with no calendar are read in the phone's, where 2026 is another year (Buddhist, Japanese, Hebrew).
- **Decided (replaces "outside the app every kind completes in one step" of the completion shapes):** a deadline closes from outside the app only when its completion's shape asks nothing the app alone can ask, declared once (`CompletionShape.closesOutsideTheApp`: no new day it goes on from, `newEnd`, and no document it replaces, `replacement`). Such a deadline (a car's kit, a contract's or a tenancy's end, a renewal of one's own, a personal document, a health card) is never closed from outside: a reminder's „Am rezolvat” opens the app at its „Am rezolvat” for that cycle, over its page, behind its section's lock (`AppRoute.completion`, a notification category of its own whose button opens the app); Siri and Shortcuts continue there; the widget offers no „Gata” for it. *Why:* closed in one step, a one-off that needs a new day closed for good and never reminded again, and a passport closed from a reminder showed as replaced, so the trip check left out a valid one. *Alternative:* leave it waiting in Urmează from every surface; not done where the surface can open the app, since the person asked to close it now.
- **Decided:** in the app, a completion that goes on from a new day needs it: „Salvează” waits, with one line above it, until the day is chosen (`CompletionDraft.lacksNewDay`).
- **Decided:** the widget's „Gata” runs in the app on iOS 26 too (`LiveActivityIntent`, which iOS runs in the app's process, started in the background; no Live Activity is involved), so `IntentEngine.perform`'s pass through the coordinator takes that deadline's pending and shown reminders away with the tap. The queue in the App Group stays for a tap the app can't take yet (the binder not openable before the phone's first unlock) and for the extension, should the system still run the intent there. *Why not from the widget:* only the coordinator schedules or removes reminders.
- **Decided:** the badge moves with the days while the app stays closed, through the reminders iOS keeps (`BadgePlanner`): every reminder carries the badge of the day it fires, and each of the next seven days whose count changes gets a reminder that only sets the badge as that day starts (nothing shown or heard), in the room the reminders leave. *Limit:* beyond the week, or when reminders fill the places iOS keeps, the badge moves with the next reminder that fires or when the app next runs (the background refresh, when iOS allows it). *Alternative:* the background refresh alone; not enough, since iOS decides when it runs.
- **Decided:** every pass clears what Notification Center shows of a deadline whose category is locked now, when the reminder named it (its category says which, `ReminderActions.isProtected(category:)`), so a lock turned on leaves nothing named there.
- **Decided:** while the whole app is locked, Siri's and Shortcuts' lists of deadlines follow the iPhone's search (`SearchPrivacy.outsideListsAnything`, the one rule for both): nothing is suggested, and a deadline a shortcut already holds comes back as „Scadență protejată”, without its day, so the shortcut still runs and opens the app.
### A share never reads a file whole for its size, and „Renunță” leaves nothing
- **Decided:** a file too large for the share is refused without being read: its stated size first, the bytes counted as it streams, and „too large” ends the item, so no other way is asked (before, the last way asked for its bytes, whole in memory, which a share extension may not survive). Once a share has no room left, the files after it aren't asked for at all. An item's bytes are asked for only when every way before said its sender had no file to give: never for a file that is there but couldn't be copied (offline, still in iCloud, not allowed), whose bytes would be that same file read whole. The item provider wraps what a sender's file gave in its own "couldn't load it", so the cause under it is now told first (`ShareFailure(_:)`): "not allowed" stays "not allowed", and only an error that says nothing more means nothing was handed over. Text that is a file (a .txt from Files) is asked for as a file first, like any other; only text with no file of its own (copied from a message) comes as bytes first. *Why:* a share extension has little memory; the size is the one thing knowable before reading. *Mitigation, said plainly:* a sender that hands over only bytes (no file, no link) hands them whole; their size is checked before anything is kept.
- **Decided:** „Renunță” stops the share at once through one token (`ShareCancellation`): nothing more is asked for, the copy under way stops between two pieces, the system's loads and coordinated reads are cancelled, the share's staging is removed, the notice isn't posted (`InboxNotice.announce(…stop:)`), and what it had already committed is taken out of the inbox; a notice already on its way then speaks of nothing that waits and the coordinator withdraws it, as for any such notice. The sheet closes once that is done (`cancelRequest`). The share's whole flow moved from the extension to `ShareDelivery`, so tests pin it. The earlier receiving (`EarlierShareReceiving`, switched off) doesn't stop early.
- **Decided:** staging a share ended mid-copy left behind (the system stopped it) goes at launch and whenever the app reads the inbox, besides the next share's start (`ShareInbox.removeAbandonedStaging`): only folders untouched for longer than a share takes (`longestShare`, an hour, from the later of when it was made and when a file last went in), so a share running at the same moment keeps its own. *Why not a lock file:* a lock held in the App Group while the extension is suspended gets it ended by the system.
- **Decided:** „Golește lista” never closes its own page: Despre's row stays while *Probleme la primire* is open, so the page and its toast stay, and goes once back in Despre with the list empty. Its „Anulează” puts back exactly what it took: each log entry has an id (`ShareFailureLog.Entry.id`, given to an earlier entry as it is read), and the merge with what came meanwhile is by id, never by value; two failures alike in the same second (the log keeps moments to the second) are two.

### Backups, kept copies and values from outside: the person's record wins, nothing collides, nothing stops
- **Decided (replaces "the copy furthest on: the later due day first…, then the one with more history" of „One copy of a household deadline”):** of two copies of the rental income declaration, the one with the person's history stays (`HouseholdRules.kept`): a filed copy first, the one furthest on (so no reminder comes back for a cycle already filed); else a copy with their document, own note, snooze or reminders they changed; else the earliest still open; then the smallest id. The others' completions and documents move onto it, and so do their note and chosen reminders where it has none of its own. *Why:* a fresh copy the app just made, due next May, beat the person's overdue copy, and its note, reminders and snooze went with it. With sync, a new declaration is made only once the binder has all arrived (`CloudArrival`), as its removal already waited: the person's own may still be on its way.
- **Decided:** every copy kept before an iCloud account change is a file of its own (`KeptCopies.name`: the day's name, then „… 2”, „… 3”), written in a hidden folder first so a copy that didn't finish is never among them. The newest three stay (`KeptCopies.mostKept`, by one order, `all(in:)`), and Settings says when each was kept. *Why three:* each copy is the whole binder, documents included, and only the latest are worth bringing back; three covers a switch and a switch back on one day. *Left open for the owner:* whether three is right, or the oldest should never go without asking.
- **Decided (adds to „The widget's symbol, the health file's own card deadline…” on the policy's facts):** a policy number that reads as the kept one but for characters recognition takes for one another (O, 0 and Q; I, 1 and L; B and 8; S and 5; Z and 2; G and 6) is the same policy misread: the number kept and a sum the person typed for it stay (`CaptureText.readAlike`). No two digits share a group, so two numbers of digits only are alike only when equal, and a renewal's new number is still another policy. The look-alikes are one list, which the machine-readable zone's repairs read too (`CaptureText.lookAlikes`). *Only a mitigation:* a number misread in another way (a 3 read as an 8, a digit lost) still reads as another policy; nothing on the page tells those apart from a renewal.
- **Decided:** a protected backup opened with nothing typed for its password asks again with its own words („Nu ai scris nicio parolă. …”, `ArchiveError.noPasswordTyped`) and keeps the copy, never „not a backup”.
- **Decided:** a reminder's lead, a grace and a travel margin are held, where they are read, to between none and a hundred years (`ReminderLead.bounded`, `longestDays`, the longest repeat): in `ReminderProfile`, which every backup, catalog and stored column comes through, and in `UrgencyRule`, which the widget's snapshot comes through. A car's year that no count holds is an unknown age (`FieldValue.wholeNumber`; its ITP every year). *Why:* the most negative lead stopped the app, and a car year of 1e19 stopped „Am rezolvat” on its ITP.
- **Decided:** checking a backup streams every file and keeps none but the manifest; bringing one in writes each file out and decodes the files that describe the household one at a time from there, each let go before the next (`DossierArchiveReader`). The limits are the one set both ways, unchanged. *Why:* checking held every data file whole, up to about 1.3 GB at the limits. *Left as it is:* a version 2 archive from the first app is converted with all its data files held, as its converter needs them together; those archives are small. A decoded data file is still held whole while it is decoded: JSON is read whole.
- **Decided:** a backup's errors say what went wrong in plain words, never a path inside the archive („O parte din copia de siguranță e deteriorată. …”); the path stays on the error for tests.
- **Decided:** „Înlocuiește tot” over two copies of one record not joined yet writes onto the copy joining keeps on every device (`RecordsMeet.staysBefore`, now the importer's rule too), so another device joining at the same moment deletes the same copy.
- **Decided:** only the open container's own iCloud reports count for sync's health and for whether the binder has arrived (`SyncEventSources`): a step that started before the open container was asked for is a retired container's; the open container's mirroring is known by its first report after that (while a retired one is still around, by its setting up, which every new mirroring starts with), and only its reports count from then on. *Why:* a download a retired container finished could pass for the new container's first download, and the steps that wait for the binder to arrive would run early.
- **Decided:** tests that mean the current schema name `CurrentSchema`; only a test about one version names it (`CaptureLearnsTests.versionSixteenOnlyAddsTheLessons`).

### A locked section is locked wherever it is named (owner)
- **Decided (owner):** Caută keeps showing the title of a locked section's deadlines and visits, with nothing else under it („Deblochează secțiunea ca să vezi mai mult.”); opening one still asks for Face ID. *Why:* finding „RCA” or „Schimb ulei” in the binder is what search is for, and a title alone is what the binder's own boxes already name; the iPhone's search stays stricter and shows nothing of a locked category.
- **Decided:** every other place that names a locked section from outside its page shows only that it is locked, by the one answer for its lock (`SectionRowLock`, drawn by `SectionRowGate`): „Act protejat” for a document, „Secțiune protejată” for any other section (`LockedWording`, which De verificat uses too), a lock and no name, expiry, badge or card digits; the row opens the section's page, which asks for Face ID. This covers a person's documents (people aren't locked, so their page opens from Caută or a link without Face ID), a home's subscriptions, a firm's „Are grijă de” and the lists behind „Vezi tot”; a person's hero (`HolderHero`); a box's line and a page's status line for a deadline of a locked section filed under an open one (`BoxStatus.line(isSensitive:isLocked:)`); and Agendă's last job (`Provider.lastJobLine(isLocked:)`), as a firm's page already did. *Alternative:* skipping locked documents in the hero and naming the next open one; not done, since it would name a later date as the next.
- **Decided:** a sheet of a locked screen lives inside its gate. A screen's own sheets are drawn behind the lock of the screen that presents them (`EnvironmentValues.screenLock`, set by `LockGate`; a deadline's page, which draws its own locked state, declares it), and „Am rezolvat” and the note sheet behind their record's section wherever they open from. When the lock comes back while one is up, it shows the locked state with *Închide*; what it held is not saved. The architecture check's `sheet-outside-its-lock` fails a screen that decides a lock itself and presents a sheet without saying which lock it lives behind. *Alternative:* closing every sheet of the page when it locks; not done, since a sheet then needs code of its own on every screen, and the gate inside the sheet works the same for every one.
- **Decided:** a document opens behind every section it belongs to (its own, its deadline's, its record's in Istoric, its part's, its reading's: `Attachment.lock(among:)`); one of the whole household's opens freely, and one that names nothing that can be found is behind Face ID while anything of the binder is locked (`RouteLock.unknownSection`). Locks fail closed.
- **Decided:** Urmează reads the locks again whenever one changes (`SectionLocks.didChange`), and „Gata” reads the lock from the deadline as it is when tapped (`Obligation.isSensitive(in:)`, the answer `IntentEngine` asks too), never from the row read before. The lock window ends typing in the scene as the cover goes up, so the keyboard never shows above it.

### „Probleme la primire” can be emptied, and a share names every file it left out (owner)
- **Decided (owner, answers "Left open for the owner" of „What didn't arrive of a share is said, counted and kept where it can be read”):** the page gets „Golește lista”, a destructive button under the list. It empties the share log (`ShareFailureLog.clear`), so the row in Despre goes until the next failure. *Why:* an old failure kept the row in Despre for good. Showing only the last few weeks was the other option; emptying keeps the person in charge and needs no rule for how old is too old.
- **Decided:** emptying asks nothing first and shows „Am golit lista.” with „Anulează”, which puts the list back (`ShareFailureLog.putBack`) beside any failure the share kept meanwhile, in time order, the latest 20. *Why an undo toast and not a question:* the copy guide prefers an undo wherever a step can be taken back, and this one can cheaply: the log is at most 20 small entries, kept in memory for the toast. Leaving the page closes the toast and the undo with it, as for every page's toast.
- **Decided:** the share extension and the app change the log only under one coordinated write each (`ShareInbox.changeFailureLog`, `NSFileCoordinator`, as the widget's queue does), read and written inside it, so a failure the share keeps while the list is emptied is never lost, and what was emptied never comes back with it. Reading is coordinated too.
- **Decided (owner):** items the share can't take (not a photo, a PDF or text) and files past the most one share takes (`ShareInbox.maxFiles`) are no longer left out without a word. When something else arrived, the confirmation's note says each on its own line with its own count and words (`ShareFailureWording`: „Nu pot primi un fișier. Pot primi poze, PDF-uri și text. …”, „3 fișiere au rămas pe dinafară. Trimite cel mult 20 de fișiere odată, apoi restul separat.”), after the files tried in vain, which keep #116's one line under the most telling reason. These two are reasons of their own (`ShareFailure.unsupportedType`, `overFileLimit`, `isLeftUntried`), never folded into a copy's failure: they are counted apart (`ShareFailure.Missed.left`). When nothing arrived, today's messages stay („Nu pot primi asta”, too large and the other reasons). Both are kept in the share log as kinds only; the files past the limit are one entry for all of them, so they never push the other failures out of the log, and an entry now says how many files it held (`Entry.files`; an earlier log reads as one each), which Despre's count adds up. A whole share is received by one shared step (`ShareReceiving.receiveAll`), so the app's tests pin what the extension counts.
- **Decided (replaces "counted under the most telling reason" of „What didn't arrive of a share is said, counted and kept where it can be read” where the files had different reasons):** a count goes only with what is true of every file it counts. Files tried in vain for one reason are told by it („2 fișiere sunt încă în iCloud. …”). Files with different reasons are told by what is true of all of them, then the most telling reason, counted by the files that had it („2 fișiere nu au ajuns. Un fișier e încă în iCloud. Nu l-am putut descărca acum. …”; `ShareFailure.Missed.Part.ofReason`, `ShareFailureWording.line`); when that reason names no cause (nothing handed over, a copy that failed), only what is true of all („Fișierele nu s-au putut copia. …”). When nothing arrived, the title says „Nu s-au trimis” and the message the counted reason (`Phase.failed(…ofReason:)`). *Why:* „2 fișiere sunt încă în iCloud.” for one file in iCloud and one failed copy said something untrue.
### The widget's symbol, the health file's own card deadline, and the follow-ups of 17 November (owner)
- **Decided (owner):** the small widget keeps the section's symbol beside the title, as built on 17 November, so its „Gata” is a full 44 pt in the smallest small widget. *Why:* a full „Gata” matters more than a line of its own for the section's name.
- **Decided (owner, answers the question left open on 17 November):** a health file keeps its own „Card european de sănătate” deadline, beside the card among the person's documents. *Why:* someone who keeps the card's day on the health file, or has no photo of the card yet, still gets reminded; closing it offers the card as a document, so the two meet there.
- **Decided (owner, replaces „Left as it is: a new policy that prints no sum leaves the earlier one” of „A policy's insured sum…” on 17 November):** a newer policy replaces the earlier policy's facts. What describes one policy and no other is declared once, on its role (`BlueprintFactRole.belongsToThePolicy`): its number and the sum it insures for. Another policy that doesn't print one of them leaves it empty (`CaptureFiler.newPolicy`), so the earlier policy's never passes for this one's; who issued it, its claims line and who it insures carry over a renewal, as they usually do, until a newer paper names others. A paper is another policy when it prints another number than the one kept, compared as values are; without two numbers to compare, when it renews the deadline („Am rezolvat”, or a review whose day comes after the deadline's). *Alternative:* clear every fact a new policy doesn't print; not done, since a renewal rarely reprints the claims line and the person would lose it each year.
- **Decided (owner):** health-card deadlines an earlier catalog made repeating every two years are fixed through the repair list (`RenewalRepeatsStep`, in *De corectat*): it stops them repeating, as the blueprint now says. It finds any deadline whose closing replaces a document and still repeats, by what its completion does (`CompletionShape.replacesDocument`), never by its name, so a personal document's renewal given a repeat is covered too. *A correction, not a suggestion:* the stored rule was saved by an earlier catalog and now says something untrue on the deadline's page; it isn't a better way of doing something that was right. Applying it changes no reminder and no due day, since closing already ignored the repeat. *Editează* no longer offers a repeat for such a deadline (`DeadlineEdit.closesWhenDone`), so it can't be given one again.
- **Decided (owner):** a code kept under another code's name before subscriptions had a billing account and a place code (#103) moves only where it is certain (`SubscriptionCodesStep`): the earlier reader kept a bill's *Cont de facturare* and *Cont client* as *Cod client*, and a water or heating bill's *Cod loc consum* as a gas CLC (never as *Cod client*: the client code's labels then held no place code). A code moves when the subscription's own papers, their kept text read again by today's reader and kept as capture keeps them for its service, name that value as exactly one other code the service has and never under the field it is in, and that code's field is empty or holds the same value. *Why no guess from the value:* a client code and a billing account look alike, and a subscription typed by hand says nothing of where its code came from; those stay as they are.
- **Decided (owner):** a home's Abonamente pocket and its full list are grouped the way the Abonamente page is, through the same listing (`SubscriptionGroup.listing`). In the pocket, which shows three, each group is named above its first row once the pocket holds more than one group; a pocket of one group shows no heading under its own title. The full list has one section per group.
- **Decided (owner):** the repeat stepper steps in the unit's own steps (`RepeatChoice.ladder`): days one by one to two weeks, then whole weeks to eight, then about a month at a time to a year; weeks one by one to twelve, then four at a time, then half a year; months one by one to twelve, then a quarter to two years, then a year; years one by one. *Why:* 14 and 21 days were out of reach. *Alternative:* typing the number; not done, since the steps now reach every common choice and VoiceOver keeps adjusting one value it reads once.

### A screenshot offers the whole page (owner)
- **Decided (owner):** a screenshot taken in the app offers the screenshot editor's *Pagină întreagă* (Full Page), the screen's whole scrolling content as a PDF, for every screen.
- **Decided:** built once, as the scene's one `UIScreenshotService` delegate (`FullPageScreenshots`, set by `FullPageScreenshotHost` in `AppShell`), never by a screen. The page is the screen in front: the topmost sheet still up, else the selected tab's top screen, and its largest scroll view that scrolls up and down and covers at least half of it (`FullPageScreenshotTarget`). It is drawn from the app's own window a screen at a time while the content scrolls underneath a still copy of the screen, then put back where it was, so the PDF is what the screen draws: the person's text size, light or dark, masked codes still masked, an iPad's split width. *Alternative:* drawing the screen's SwiftUI content again off the screen; not done, since a screen's views can't be rebuilt generically outside their place, and a lazy list draws only the rows near the screen either way.
- **Decided:** nothing is offered while a lock covers the app (`LockCover`, the shell's one answer; the lock window is in the same scene, so the same delegate answers for it), while a locked state shows (`.withholdsFullPageScreenshot()`, on `DSLockedState` and a locked deadline's card), for an alert, for a screen without a scroll view, or for content that fits on the screen. A capture covered on its way gives nothing. Then only the plain screenshot exists.
- **Decided:** a very long list stops at thirty screens (`FullPageScreenshot.mostScreens`), from the top while the visible part is among them, else ending with it: each screen is scrolled to, drawn and kept as a picture while the editor waits. PDF pages are at most 14,400 pt tall (`largestPageSide`, the PDF reference's limit), and the editor opens at the visible part (`current`). The PDF is made in memory; no file is written.
- **Pinned by:** `FullPageScreenshotTests` (when it is offered, which scroll view, the stretch, the pages and the place the editor opens, a capture in a window that puts the list back). The editor itself shows only on a device or the simulator.
### What didn't arrive of a share is said, counted and kept where it can be read (owner)
- **Decided (owner, the follow-ups of #100):** when some files of one share arrive and others don't, the share sheet's one confirmation says what arrived and, under it, how many didn't, the most telling reason and what to do (`ShareConfirmation.note`, from `ShareFailure.Tally`), never a second message. Every reason is counted by the files it is about, with the String Catalog's plurals: one „Fișierul…”, several „Fișierele…” (`ShareFailureWording`, the one place those words live). Setări › Despre gets *Probleme la primire*: the share log's latest failures in plain words, when and why, each with what to do, so a „Nu s-a trimis” can be explained without a developer. *Why:* the log was written but nobody could see it, and a partly failed share looked like a full success.
- **Decided:** the row shows only while the log holds something (Settings keeps fewer rows); the page says „Nicio problemă la primire.” when it is empty. The page uses the share sheet's own words for one file, never a name or path (the log keeps none), and leaves out the way tried and the error's code, which say nothing to the person. *Left open for the owner:* the log keeps its latest 20 entries with no way to clear them, so an old failure keeps the row in Despre; options are a „Golește lista” action, or showing only the last few weeks.
### Capture learns where an issuer prints a value, by the page's positions
- **Decided (replaces "learned labels are told to the readers as the kind's table for that read, before its own rows" of „Capture learns from what the person saved”):** what was learned of an issuer is read where the issuer prints each value: by the label reader (`LabelledLayout`, the one that reads identity documents by position) over the page's lines as recognized, each where it sits, never joined into rows (`CaptureAnalyzer.addLearned`). A value beside its label is read anywhere along its row (`LessonBook.rowReach`, where an identity card's reach is eight line heights), else under the label in its column; each value is read as its field is (`LabelledLayout.field`: a day by the one date reader, an amount, a number by `RomanianNumber`, text as printed, a value printed in parts whole). Learned labels are no longer told to the kind's line readers, so a lesson is read one way. They are read before the kind's readers and worth more than the kind's own rows (`LessonBook.readingConfidence`, 0.85), since a lesson exists only where those readers missed or misread this issuer's value; a check still wins (a CUI's control digit). *Why:* the kind's line readers work on rows joined across the page, so on a two-column bill or policy („Data emiterii” and „Termen achitare” over their two dates) they take the wrong column's value, and a lesson could only be a label before its value on its line or the line above.
- **Decided:** the learner finds a value's label where the label reader would read the value, backwards (`LabelledLayout.lineBefore`, `lineAbove`, the same rules as `beside` and `column`): the words before it on its line, after a date or an amount printed before it (`CaptureLessonLearner.afterLastValue`); else, on a page with positions, the nearest line to its left on its row when it ends in a label, else the nearest line above it in its column; on text without positions, the line above, as before. Every safeguard stays: one label for the value wherever it is printed, one to four words, no digits, none of the values saved, no card number or CNP, read again on the same page with the lesson, and nothing the phone read right read differently. A bare number before the value is part of the label („Termen 30 zile”), which is then refused for its digit; only a value a reader reads by itself (a date, an amount with its currency) ends another label's value.
- **Decided:** a wording the kind reads as another value (`CaptureKindProfile.wordings(otherThan:)`) is never a lesson. One the kind reads as the same value (`wordings(of:)`) is learned only where the kind's own readers, without any lesson, read that value wrong on the page: the kind's own bill rows are still read on joined rows, so a two-column bill whose due date sits under the kind's own „Data scadentă” is read right once its issuer's paper teaches it, and a wording the kind already reads right is never learned again. *Only a mitigation for the kind's own rows:* reading the kind's tables by position too (`BillReader`, the line readers) is the cause's fix; not done here, since the line readers' joined rows read far-apart label and value on one row today, and reading them by position needs checking against real bills first.
- **Decided (checked again, still true):** number and date formats aren't learned per issuer. Every number on a document is read by one rule, the owner's (`RomanianNumber`: a comma is the decimal mark, a dot groups thousands, a number with one mark and three digits after it follows the Romanian rule and is marked „Verifică”), and every date by one reader, day first, in each form a Romanian paper prints (`RomanianFieldExtractor.dates`). There is nothing to choose between for an issuer, and learning „this issuer means 3.750 by 3,750” would be a second rule beside the owner's; a doubtful number stays marked for the person to look at.
### Whether anything of the binder is locked is one answer
- **Decided:** whether any section is behind Face ID on this device, and which categories are, is `BinderLocks`: the one answer for a section's lock (`CategoryTemplate.isSensitive(key:template:in:)`) asked for every category whose record is here and for every category a section names, so a section that arrives before its category counts as locked, as on its own page. The app-switcher cover, Face ID before a backup or bringing one in, Settings' *Protecție* row and De verificat read it; a binder that can't be read counts as locked. *Why:* every place that asks whether anything is locked gives the answer the section's own page gives, from one source. The cover reads it only as the app leaves the screen with the session open (`LockCover.decide` takes it as an autoclosure), so the shell no longer follows every category to draw itself. *Pinned by:* `LockedSectionsEverywhereTests`, `LockCoverDecisionTests`, and the `lock-without-its-category` check, which now refuses category records filtered by their lock (`\.isSensitive`, `{ $0.isSensitive }`).

## 2026-11-17

### A policy's insured sum, a full „Gata” in the widget, the health card closed like a document (owner)
- **Decided (owner):** the sum a policy insures for is kept on the policy, as one of its deadline's own facts (`BlueprintFactRole.insuredSum`, read as money by `DeadlineFacts`), declared once for every policy that states one: a home's PAD (*Sumă asigurată PAD*) and optional policy (*Sumă asigurată facultativă*), and a car's CASCO (*Sumă asigurată CASCO*). *Why, in the owner's words:* it matters more for optional insurance than for the PAD, but both should have it. An RCA keeps none: its limits are set by law, and its blueprint never listed one. Capture already read it (#105); now it files like the insurer and the number, into the fields of the deadline the policy files into, from the review and from „Am rezolvat”, and a newer policy's sum replaces the one kept. The deadline's page shows it under *Polița*, after the number. *No schema change:* the sum is a section value, stored with the others (`KeptObject`); catalog 26 brings the fields. *Left as it is:* a new policy that prints no sum leaves the earlier one, as for the claims phone; the review offers *Sumă asigurată* to add.
- **Decided (owner):** the widget's „Gata” is a full 44 pt tall and at least as wide in every size. *The trade-off the owner accepted:* the medium widget shows two deadlines instead of three. How many each family shows is declared once (`WidgetLayout.rows(in:)`, the timeline keeping the most, `rowLimit`), and „Gata”'s look once (`WidgetDoneLabel`, its capsule drawn on its 44 pt label, since a bordered button adds padding of its own). The small widget puts the section's symbol beside the title, as the Lock Screen's rectangle does, so the title, the section and when keep their text sizes above a full „Gata” in the smallest small widget. `WidgetLayoutTests` measures „Gata” at every text size and checks both widgets' lines fit the smallest widget an iPhone on iOS 26 draws. The Lock Screen families are unchanged.
- **Decided (owner, replaces "The European health card's deadline on a health section keeps *Cardul nou* and *Expiră pe*" of „Closing a deadline keeps every deadline right”):** the European health card closes like a personal document: a new card has a new number, like a passport. As a personal document it already did (its category's one renewal, `CompletionKind.documentReplaced`). On a health file, its deadline (`CompletionKind.newHealthCard`) now declares a replacement too, of one of the holder's documents rather than of the section (`CompletionShape.Replacement.Replaces.holdersDocument`, with the card's `DocumentType`): „Am rezolvat” asks only *Când*, closes the deadline and keeps it in Istoric; the closed deadline's page offers *Adaugă cardul nou*, the personal-document add flow for the file's person, until a card of that type made after the closing is among their documents (`DocumentPageFacts.hasSuccessor(of:closing:)`), and the toast offers *Vezi termenul*. *Why not `documentReplaced` itself on the health file:* that kind replaces its section, so the whole health file would read *Expirat* and offer *Șterge actul vechi*, deleting the person's medical data. A deadline whose closing replaces a document closes whatever its repeat (`ObligationActions.complete`), so health-card deadlines an earlier catalog made repeating every two years close too; the blueprint no longer repeats (catalog 26). The trip check, which lists the card as optional, then no longer counts the closed deadline and counts the new card once it is in the binder, never a replaced one (`Asset.wasReplaced`, `isCurrentDocument`). *Left open for the owner:* whether a health file should still offer its own health-card deadline at all, now that the card lives among the person's documents.
### The privacy policy and help pages: GitHub Pages from `site/`
- **Decided (owner):** the privacy policy and support pages App Store Connect asks for (guideline 5.1.1(i)) are GitHub Pages from this public repository, not a host of their own. *Why:* no server, account or cost of ours, the text lives beside the code it describes, and every version of the policy is public in the repository's history.
- **Decided:** the site is its own folder, `site/` (plain HTML and one stylesheet, no scripts, no web fonts, no trackers; a content security policy allows only its own files), published by `.github/workflows/pages.yml` with the Pages setting **Source: GitHub Actions**. *Alternative:* Pages from a branch, which serves only the root or `docs/`; `docs/` holds the project's own documents, which would then be published as pages beside the policy. Until Pages is turned on, the workflow says so and publishes nothing.
- **Decided:** Romanian first, each page with an English version: `confidentialitate/` and `ajutor/`, `en/privacy/` and `en/help/`. The policy says only what the app does today, from AGENTS.md (sections 4 and 6), SPEC and the privacy manifests; where something depends on the build (iCloud sync), it says „if sync is on”, true before and after sync ships. It speaks of the app and its developer, never „noi”, and names no one.
- **Decided:** help goes through the repository's GitHub Issues (a template of its own, *Ajutor sau o problemă*, which asks for no personal data), security problems through GitHub's private vulnerability reporting, as SECURITY.md says. No email address is published; if App Store review asks for one, the owner adds it.
- **Decided:** Settings › Despre opens *Politica de confidențialitate* and *Ajutor*; their addresses are written once, in `PublicPages`, and `PublicPagesTests` checks that each is a page `site/` serves and that every link inside the site leads to a page it has. Despre's sentence says „îl folosesc”, as the app speaks („eu”), where it said „îl folosim”.

### A replaced document is never the person's current one
- **Decided:** whether a personal document is still its holder's current one has one answer, `Asset.wasReplaced` (its renewal closed with „Am rezolvat”, `DocumentPageFacts.replacement`), and every reader that picks, counts or orders documents reads it. The readiness checks carry it in their snapshot (`ReadinessAsset.wasReplaced`, `isCurrentDocument`) and pick every document through `ReadinessEngine.documents(of:in:)`, which keeps only current ones: *Pleci din țară*'s identity document and passport, the driving licence, the European health card and the green card never take a replaced one, whatever its printed date says, and documents filed under no one travel only while one of them is current. The person's hero reads `DocumentPageFacts.nextToExpire`; every list of a person's documents (their Acte, *Vezi tot*, the Acte box) shows the current ones first and the replaced ones after them (`DocumentPageFacts.currentFirst`), never among them by a date that no longer counts. *Why:* *Pleci din țară* called a replaced passport valid by its printed date, and chose it over the new one when that one lasted less (a temporary passport after a lost one).
- **Decided:** what a document says of its holder outlives the document, so a replaced one still counts there: the traveller's citizenship (`Citizenship.of`, not asked again while the new document isn't in the binder) and who a policy insures by the CNP on their documents (`Policyholders`). Urmează, the widgets, the badge, reminders, Siri and Spotlight's deadlines read deadlines, and the replaced document's renewal is closed, so they show nothing of it. Search and Spotlight's sections find every section, a replaced one too, as history, and say nothing of its validity. The sale and the claim checklists pick no personal document.
- **Pinned by:** `ReplacedDocumentsTests`, which lists every reader. No architecture rule: documents are reached by their category's part and then by their holder or a fetch, so no pattern in one line tells a reader that picks a document from one that doesn't. *Alternative:* leave the replaced documents out of the readiness snapshot altogether; not done, since the citizenship read from them would be asked again.

### Subscriptions by what they are: their codes, their groups and weekly bills
- **Decided (owner):** what a contract or a bill is identified by comes from its service, declared once as data (`ServiceKind.Service.codes`, `SubscriptionCode`): electricity the POD; gas the CLC (or *POD gaz*); water and heating a place code; internet, TV and a phone plan the client code, the contract number and the billing account (and a phone plan its number). The capture review, the subscription's edit form, `ContractReader`, `SubscriptionMatcher` and the copy matrix all read it, so a service is never asked for a code it doesn't have. *Why:* an internet and TV contract was asked for a POD or CLC, because every contract asked for both.
- **Decided (owner):** subscriptions can be weekly (*Săptămânal*), wherever a billing cycle is chosen and shown: the form, the monthly cost (a week's price × 52 / 12), the missing bill (a third of the cycle's grace, three days for a weekly bill) and its note (*n-a venit săptămâna asta*). A deadline's own repeat, *Factura de plătit*'s included, already offered weeks.
- **Decided (owner):** Abonamente lists subscriptions under what they are: *Utilități* (electricity, gas, water, heating, sanitation), *Internet și telefon* (internet, TV, mobile), *Transport*, *Aplicații și streaming*, *Altele*. Each service declares its group once; the person can move a subscription to another group from its edit form (*Grup în Abonamente*). The group is the subscription's own stored value, worked out from the service when it is saved (the `serviceGroup` derivation), so a newer catalog never moves it; changing the service moves it to that service's group, as a new service is a new thing. Grouping extends the category's existing listing (`CategoryBehavior.Listing.byMonthlyCost`, for a category that keeps the group field) rather than adding a listing or a box grouping of its own. *Alternative:* keep only the person's choice and work the rest out on display, which leaves the form's choice empty for nearly every subscription.
- **Decided (from the owner's telecom contract summary):** the EU contract summary („Fișa de sinteză”), the same headings for every provider, reads as a contract: the provider, the service, its date as the start, *Durata, reînnoirea și încetarea contractului* for the minimum period (its end worked out and reminded), *taxa de abonament* for the monthly price (never a discount line). A telecom contract reads its client code, contract number, billing account and installation address.
- New services: *Căldură*, *Salubritate* (both under a home), *Transport*, *Aplicație sau streaming*. A code a service doesn't have is dropped from what was read, unless it is the same kind of code the service names another way (a gas bill's POD is its CLC); a subscription saved before keeps every value it has.
- **Decided:** catalog 25 (after 24, the completions and renewals change), for the subscription template's codes by service, its group, its new services and the weekly cycle.

### One „Adaugă un document”, a switch for notifications, optional checks (owner's TestFlight feedback)
- **Decided (owner):** a person's Acte has one button, „Adaugă un document”, photo first (`PocketAddition.document`, opening `AddPersonalDocumentFlow` with `HolderDocumentFiling.captureDecides`). Capture decides: a personal document (`CaptureKindProfile.isPersonalDocument`) becomes its own section under them, with reminders; any other paper (a diploma, a contract) is kept on their page. „Scriu eu” stays in the flow, for typing one of their acts in by hand, and „Din Fișiere” joins it. Every „Adaugă un act” button or menu item, in every category, becomes „Adaugă un document” (`PocketWording.addDocument`). *Why, in the owner's words:* "Documente can include Acte while Acte typically doesn't include Documente. Documente can be diploma de licenta, which is not an Act." *Way back:* the two buttons it replaced („Adaugă un act”, „Scanează un document”) stay, switched off (`PocketAddition.offersOneDocumentButton`), until the owner has seen the one button on a phone. A checklist's missing passport and the Acte box still file what they read as one of the person's acts.
- **Decided (owner):** once iOS allows notifications, Settings shows a switch, „Notificări”, where the status line was. Off stops everything the app sends to this phone at once: `SideEffectsCoordinator` plans no reminder, removes the pending ones and the shown ones, clears the badge and withdraws the share's notice; on brings them back on the next pass. iOS's own permission is left as it is, and with it refused the row still says how to turn it on in the phone's Settings. Where reminders are set up, „Notificările sunt oprite” offers „Pornește-le”. *Where it lives:* a choice of this device's, like the permission iOS keeps per device, so it is an `AppPreferences` setting (`notificationsOn`), never household data, never synced or in a backup; kept once, in the App Group's defaults (`NotificationSwitch`), because the share extension reads it too: with it off the share posts no notice („Am primit 2 acte”) and its sheet says to open the app. *The widget does not follow it:* the widget is something the person placed on their screen to look at, not something the app sends, so it keeps showing what is coming; Spotlight follows the binder as before.
- **Decided (owner):** a checklist's check says whether the situation needs it or only invites it (`ReadinessNeed`, on every `ReadinessItem`, for every situation alike). An optional check that isn't ready is `ReadinessStatus.suggested`, whatever its rule found: neutral, a dashed plus, „Opțional” beside its title, an invitation in its words, never counted among what needs the person or isn't in the binder, so a checklist with only invitations left is ready. Optional today: the service history and service invoices for a sale, the European health card for a trip, the policy's photo for a claim. *Why:* „Facturi de service” showed a warning with „Fă poze facturilor pe care le ai.”, as if a sale needed them; it doesn't.
- **Decided (owner):** „Vinzi mașina” groups „Pentru cumpărător” and „De achitat înainte”; a claim's joint report says „Dacă nu vă înțelegeți, mergi la Poliție” (a minor crash with no one hurt is never a call to 112, which can be fined); a home's claim photographs „Fiecare zonă afectată”. No other situation advised calling the police or 112. Both situations' checklists are now in `docs/COPY_MATRIX.md`.
- **Decided:** every number a `String(localized:)` or a `LocalizedStringResource(…)` writes passes the app's locale (`locale: .dosarDefault`), so a count reads „1.250” whatever the phone's region; `scripts/plural-check.py` fails one that doesn't, from what the build found (its self-test holds the cases). A `Text` takes the screen's locale, already the app's. *Alternative:* passing a formatted text instead of the integer, which loses the plural forms.

### Closing a deadline keeps every deadline right
- **Decided (owner):** a personal document (an identity card, a passport, a licence, every `DocumentType`) is never renewed by moving its date: "you don't get a new expiry date when you renew it, you get an entirely new document with a new serial number, etc, so that's a new upload." Its renewal states its own completion, declared in the catalog (`CompletionKind.documentReplaced`, catalog 24), never a test of the type: „Am rezolvat” asks *Când* and *Cât a costat*, no new expiry and no paper, closes the deadline and keeps it in Istoric; the document's page then shows *Expirat* whatever its printed date (`Asset.wasReplaced`, read by its values, the person's list of documents and the person's hero, which leaves it out of the next to expire) and opens with *Ai reînnoit actul*, offering *Adaugă actul nou* (the personal-document add flow for the same person, until the new one is in the binder, `DocumentPageFacts.hasSuccessor`) and *Șterge actul vechi* (the page's own delete, with *Anulează*); the closed deadline's page shows the same card and the toast offers *Vezi actul*. The words are the completion kind's (`CompletionShape.Replacement`) and in the copy matrix. A new expiry typed on the old document later makes a new renewal, and it reads as before. Contracts and leases keep *Contractul nou* and *Se termină pe*: a contract is extended. The European health card's deadline on a health section keeps *Cardul nou* and *Expiră pe*: it repeats on that section and replaces nothing there. *Existing deadlines:* no repair. A deadline's completion is read from its category's blueprint, which catalog 24 brings at launch, so every open document renewal asks the new way at once; renewals closed or moved the old way stay as they were. *Alternative:* a `DataRepairs` step closing renewals the old way moved; not done, since nothing tells a moved date from a corrected one.
- **Decided:** closing with a new day is one action (`ObligationActions.complete(continuingOn:)`): the completion goes into Istoric and the deadline goes on from that day, repeating or not. **Why:** „Trusă medicală și stingător” and one-off deadlines of one's own (renewal, insurance, legal) closed with a new day lost it: the deadline closed, so nothing reminded about the new kit. A deadline whose date a field drives (a contract's end) now writes its completion too, and the field takes the day; this replaces "a completion that a document's expiry field drives writes no entry in Istoric" (2026-11-16, *Left open*).
- **Decided:** a new end that isn't after today is refused in the form, under its field, as a fix („Alege o zi de după azi. Data aceasta a trecut deja.”), and *Salvează* waits (`ObligationActions.continues`, the one rule; `CompletionDraft.newDayProblem`). **Why:** it was dropped without a word. A new kit or contract that already ended is almost always a slip of the picker, and saving it would close the deadline or remind at once; the copy guide asks a field to say how to fix it. A date read off a paper that has passed is not a new day: the sheet doesn't promise it and the deadline goes to its next cycle. *Alternative:* accept it and remind at once.
- **Decided:** every way of closing passes the cycle the person saw, as `IntentEngine` does, through the one completion path (`ObligationActions.complete(cycle:)`, `isOpen`, checked in the context and in the store): Urmează's row (its due day), the deadline's page and „Am rezolvat” (the cycle it opened for). A second activation (VoiceOver twice, a swipe and the menu) closes nothing more and says nothing over the first toast (`UpcomingActions.quietFailureToast`); a sheet whose cycle was closed meanwhile (a reminder, the widget, another device) files nothing and says so above *Salvează*. Locks stay out of it: a Face ID check wraps `CompletionEntry.start` and `IntentEngine` from outside.
- **Decided:** the visit form asks before closing whenever its draft differs from a new one in any value (`VisitDraft.hasContent`, compared whole), so a counter typed, a firm chosen or a rating count, and a value added to the draft later counts by itself; only the day alone, the greyed estimate and blanks don't. `VisitDiscardGuardTests` lists every value and fails on a new one until it says which it is.

### Reminders, repeats and routes, at their causes
- **Decided:** a deadline has one reminder per moment. When two of its reminders fall at the same minute (a snooze that ends on a lead's day or on the day after the due day, the catch-up on a lead's day, two leads on one day), the plan keeps one by one order (`ReminderPlanner.Purpose`): the lead, then the follow-up, the catch-up, the snooze. **Why:** „Amână 3 zile” on 17 November for a deadline due on the 20th, reminding on the day, sent the same notice twice at 09:00. Two deadlines at the same minute still each have their own.
- **Decided:** a reminder time the clocks skip on the morning summer time starts (03:30 in Romania, when 03:00 becomes 04:00) comes at the first minute after the gap, 04:00, as iOS itself does for a time that doesn't exist; a time shown twice when summer time ends comes the first time. The rule is the one place a day becomes a moment (`CalendarDay.moment(atMinute:in:)`), and a time picker set to such a time says so under it (`ReminderClock.springForward`, „Pe 28 martie, când se trece la ora de vară, îți amintesc la 04:00.”), in a deadline's *Ora amintirii* and in Settings. **Why:** it fired at 04:30 with nothing said.
- **Decided:** the side effects run their usual pass when midnight passes, the phone's time zone changes or its clock is set (`SideEffectsCoordinator.timeChanges`: `NSCalendarDayChanged`, `NSSystemTimeZoneDidChange`, `UIApplication.significantTimeChangeNotification`), and each pass reads the app's calendar as it starts instead of the one it was made with. **Why:** after a flight the badge, the widget's „Azi” and the reminders stayed on the old time zone until the app was opened again.
- **Decided (replaces "counted from its due dates, one Gata closes one cycle" of „Recurring deadlines, checked end to end”):** counted from its due dates and done after its next due day had come, a deadline moves to the first of the series' days after the day it was done (`RecurrenceEngine.nextOfSeries`, the series' day of the month kept, a late step still one of the series for `seriesStart`), so „Gata” never leaves it late the moment it is done. Fixed dates follow the same rule. The exception is declared by the kind: a payment or a tax (`ObligationKind.missedCyclesStayOwed`), whose every cycle is money still owed (a month's rent, a tax installment), keeps closing one cycle at a time. **Why:** a check or a chore counted from its due day and done late was overdue again right after „Gata”. *Alternative:* the old rule for every kind, which keeps a missed payment in sight but makes a late check look late twice; or skipping for payments too, which would hide a rent still owed.
- **Decided:** a route never lands under a sheet. Every sheet and full-screen cover a screen presents is one the router knows (`dsSheet`, `dsFullScreenCover`, `dsPresents` beside a photo or file picker, the `sheet-outside-router` check), and a route that comes while one is up closes them from the top, each once the one above has finished closing, then lands (`ShellSheetQueue.arrive`). While one holds changes the person hasn't saved (its `dsDiscardGuard`, the shell's own sheet included) or must be answered first (the notification question before a save, onboarding), the route waits until the person has closed them. **Why:** a notification or a search result tapped while a screen's own sheet was open pushed its screen under the sheet. *Alternative:* always close, which loses what the person typed; or always wait, which makes a tap from outside seem to do nothing.
- **Decided:** a deadline's *Editează* explains the notification question only when the edit adds or changes its reminders or their time (`DeadlineEdit.changesReminders`), not for a new name, day or repeat.
- **Decided:** the hand-written plural check sees any count compared with one or two that chooses between two wordings (`== 1 ?`, `> 1 ?`, `< 2 ?`), on one line or across a ternary's lines; something said only for several (a wording or nothing) is not a plural. The four it found are the String Catalog's plurals now, the number left out where the words don't say it: the share's notice („…ca să-l pui la locul lui.” / „…ca să le pui la locul lor.”), *Ca poză* / *Ca poze*, the snooze's *Mâine* / *Peste 3 zile*, and a missing bill's „n-a venit luna asta” for a monthly one, „n-a venit încă” otherwise.

### Capture learns from what the person saved
