- **Decided (owner):** capture learns from what the person saved, so the phone's own reader gets better at each issuer and a paper a model once had to read is read the next time on the phone alone; built before the App Store release. Lessons sync across the person's devices with the binder.
- **Decided:** only from a review the person saved, never from a model's answer by itself: the values saved are compared with what tiers 1–2 read from the same page, with the lessons they had then (`CaptureLessonLearner`). „Am rezolvat” and the visit form learn nothing, since their papers' values aren't each shown before saving. A value the person typed teaches like one they kept.
- **Decided:** a lesson is one label: this issuer (its CUI, else a provider the phone knows by name, else the company printed as the issuer: `IssuerKey`) prints this value of this kind of paper under these words. It is kept only when the evidence points one way: the value is printed next to exactly one label; the label has one to four words, no digits, none of the values saved, no card number and nothing shaped like a CNP; told to the readers it reads the value again on that page and changes nothing the phone read right; no lesson gives that label to another value (both go when one does). A lesson that read a value the person corrected goes. Personal documents and talons teach nothing. **Not built:** lessons about a number's or a date's format: the readers have one way to read each (`RomanianNumber`, the one date reader), so there is nothing to choose between per issuer.
- **Decided:** learned labels are told to the readers as the kind's table for that read, before its own rows (`CaptureKindProfile.learnedLabels` by `LessonBook.teaching`), never as reading code of their own; `ReadingArbiter` still decides. The lessons a page was read with go with its reading (`CaptureReadingSource.lessons`), so reading it again as another kind reads with the same, and what the phone read with them is what saving learns from.
- **Decided:** one record per lesson (`IssuerLesson`, database version 16, a lightweight stage), whose id is the lesson's own (a name-based UUID of issuer, kind, field and label). Two devices that learn the same lesson make records with the same id, which `RecordsMeet` joins as it joins any record; what two devices learned differently stands side by side (the union); a label two values claim is dropped on every device alike by an app-made step (`IssuerLessons.settle`, after `RecordsMeet` at launch and with every synced change, and inside every import). *Alternative:* one record per issuer holding a list, which needs its own merge for sync, backups and undo.
- **Decided:** a paper's lessons are saved in the filing's own unit of work, not after it (`afterCommit`): the paper and what it taught are kept or lost together, and *Anulează* takes both back. The learning is worked out before the unit opens, as the unit takes only quick synchronous work.
- **Decided:** lessons travel in backups (`data/issuer_lessons.json`, a ninth data file; older archives have none): they hold an issuer's words and layout, nothing of this phone's, so only `copyID` stays on the phone, as for every record. A backup's lesson whose label isn't one (digits, more than four words) stays out. „Înlocuiește tot” removes lessons the backup doesn't have, like any record.
- **Decided:** Settings › *Ce am învățat*, in the app's own voice (as „O păstrez în dosar”), beside *Citire cu AI*: each issuer with its labels („Data scadenței: „Ultima zi de plată””), swiped or from its menu to forget, with *Anulează*. Its status is „Nimic încă” or „De la 3 firme”.

### Numbers, agreement and passed days in the app's words
- **Decided:** a number with its unit is written one way (`MeasurementUnit.written`, `after(_:value:)`): a symbol as it is („10.000 km”), a counted word counted like the leu from the String Catalog's plurals („1 cafea”, „2 cafele”, „20 de cafele”, „24 de luni”), on one line with a no-break space, „de” and the unit included (in `Money` too). *Why:* „La fiecare 300 cafele” and „24 luni” read wrong, and a number put straight into a `String(localized:)` takes the phone's region („10,000”). *Rules that catch the class:* `scripts/plural-check.py` fails a catalog entry whose „other” form counts without „de”; `CopyMatrixTests` fails a matrix with digits grouped by a comma or a number of 20 or more before a counted word without „de”.
- **Decided:** a service of a category of one's own stores no note; where it is offered it says its rule, worked out each time (`ObligationBlueprint.shownNote`), and a deadline made from it keeps no copy (`deadlineNote`): the deadline's page says how it repeats. Copies earlier versions kept are offered for removal (`ServiceNotesStep`, asking first); a note the person wrote or changed stays. *Why:* the stored note was written with the phone's region and stopped being true once how often changed. *Alternative:* rewriting the stored notes with the right digits; still wrong after the next change of interval.
- **Decided:** the app speaks as one („Îți amintesc”, never „îți amintim”), in every empty line; words that agree with a gender are said only of what declares one, so *Bani*'s total is „În total” on every page, a category's name is never treated as a plural, and a paper's line in Acte is „Expiră pe…” / „A expirat pe…”. The electronic signature's sentences agree with its title („Unde o pun”, „În certificat scrie”), and the noun-and-title rule now checks the noun's head word.
- **Decided:** a day already passed is said in passed words and never promises a reminder before it; a deadline made for it says it shows in Urmează. Who a renewed paper comes from is declared with the paper (`CompletionShape.Paper.origin`): a contract or a lease is signed („Cu cine l-ai semnat”), a document issued.
- **Decided:** catalog 23 (after 22, the trips and citizenship change), for the built-ins' words that changed (the minimum period's note, the people category's summary, „gaze”, „Se plătește prin debit direct”).

### A PAD is read like the other policies
- **Decided (owner's report: reading a PAD was poor):** a digital PDF is read by where its lines are printed (`PDFTextLines`), like a photo's lines, instead of its plain text, whose order is the producer's: a form often writes every label first and every value after them, so a policy's period, sums and holder were far from their labels. A row where a run of labels and the values in its gaps cross is read again word by word where each is printed; when that doesn't spell the row exactly, the row stays as PDFKit gives it. The plain text is still the reading when a PDF gives no lines. *Alternative:* recognizing a digital PDF's rendered pages like a scan, slower and less exact than the text the PDF carries.
- **Decided:** a PAD's insurer is PAID, whichever insurer sold it (Legea 260/2008), as data on the cover (`InsuranceCover.soleInsurers`); the seller printed beside it (*prin: …*) is never taken for it, and the tax ID read is the one printed with the insurer's own name. The catalog names each insurer by the words its papers use (`InsuranceProviderItem.words`), PAID also by its full name.
- **Decided:** a policy also gives the sum it insures for (`insuredSum`) and the home it insures (`insuredAddress`), and the number of the policy it renews is never its own (`renewedPolicy`, a label only). A home's policy goes to the home at that address by the rule bills follow (`SubscriptionMatcher.home(at:among:)`), and a home without an address takes it; an address the person wrote is never changed. The sum is shown in the review and kept with the document's text, not as a field of the home (the home's template has none); adding one is the owner's call.

### Several trips, and travel rules that follow citizenship
- **Decided (owner):** several trips are kept at once, not one. Each has its own days, region, destination and who goes, is kept on this phone until the day after it ends and then drops away by itself (`TripPlanStore`, a list under a new key; the one trip an earlier version kept is brought across and its old place removed). The privacy rule for the destination stays (`SearchPrivacy.mayRemember`). In a household of several people each trip names who goes: from a member's page the screen is about the trips they go on, from Urmează about everyone's; with one such trip its checklist opens at once, with more the list of them, with none the questions. *Toate călătoriile*, *Altă călătorie* and *Șterge călătoria* (asked first) are in the trip's *Mai multe*. This replaces "the trip is kept on this phone … until the day after it ends" (2026, *The readiness screens*) for its single trip only; it is still not a record. *Alternatives:* opening on the list of trips every time (one more tap for the usual single trip); a trip in the binder (a database version, synced and backed up), not asked for.
- **Decided (owner):** inside the EU an EU citizen needs an identity card or a passport, either one; the check accepts whichever is valid and never asks for one of them by name (*Carte de identitate sau pașaport* when neither is in the binder). This replaces "inside the EU an identity card is enough … and a passport stands in for it" in its wording only; the identity card is still preferred when both are valid.
- **Decided (owner):** a person has a citizenship, kept on them in their values (`MemberFields.citizenship`, a choice of countries by ISO code, `country.RO`, named in Romanian from the app's locale, with *Fără cetățenie (apatrid)*; catalog version 22, no database change: the values already hold it). It is read from their passport's or residence permit's machine-readable zone and their identity card's citizenship line (`DocumentFields.nationality`, `Citizenship.key(forNationality:)`), kept on them when a document is filed and they have none yet, and changed on their page; one they have is never changed by a document. Where none is kept, the trip check reads it from their documents (a free-movement citizenship first, for a person with two). With none known it asks once, in plain words (*Spune-mi ce cetățenie are, ca să știu ce acte îi trebuie.*), and never assumes Romanian.
- **Decided (owner):** the travel rules follow the citizenship and are declared once as data (`TravelRules`, by `Citizenship.Group`: free movement for the EU's 27, the EEA and Switzerland; any other country or none; not known): an EU, EEA or Swiss citizen travels in the EU with an identity card or a passport; a citizen of a country outside the EU living in Romania needs a passport and a valid residence permit, inside the EU and outside it (the permit is what lets them back in); outside the EU everyone needs a passport good for travel until the trip ends, with a reminder to look up the destination's visa rules. There is no visa database. Not known: the citizenship is asked first; inside the EU no document is counted meanwhile, outside it the passport is.
- **Decided (owner):** *Permis de ședere* is a personal document type (`DocumentType.residencePermit`), with an expiry and reminders like the others (three months ahead and 45 days before it ends, since its extension is asked for at least 30 days before), its back kept, its renewal's papers, offered wherever a person's documents are added, and read from a photo: an `IdentityTemplate` with the EU uniform format's labels and a TD1 zone. A zone's document code names the kind by data on the templates (`machineReadableCodes`: "P", "I", "IR", the longest first), no longer "a passport or else an identity card" in the classifier.
- **Decided:** a choice field with a long list (more than `DSChoiceField.menuLimit`, 24) opens a searchable list in a sheet (`DSChoiceList`), not a menu; the trip check's question uses the same list. *Alternative:* a menu of some 200 countries.
- **Why:** a household with a second trip lost the first, a non-Romanian member was told an identity card would do, and the check named an identity card where a passport is as good.


### A locked section behaves the same on every surface
- **Decided:** whether a section is locked never depends on its category's record being there. One answer, `CategoryTemplate.isSensitive(key:template:in:)` (`Asset.isSensitive(template:)`, `vault.isLocked(asset, template:)`): the record's lock when it is here; without it (its category still coming from iCloud or an import), this device's choice for the key, else the catalog's default for a built-in, else locked. Every screen, Caută, Spotlight, the widget, reminders, Siri and the filing read it. The `lock-without-its-category` check stops a missing record from reading as open (`isSensitive ?? false`, `?.isSensitive == true`, a vault check of an optional template defaulted to false). *Alternative:* treat an unknown category as open until it arrives, as before; rejected, as locks fail closed.
- **Decided:** every route and every sheet says which lock it opens behind, in a switch of its own (`AppRoute.lock`, `ShellSheet.lock`, a `RouteLock`), and the shell draws each behind it (`RouteLockGate`), wherever it was opened from: a tap, a reminder, a widget, a `dosarcusina://` link, a search result. A visit's link (`dosarcusina://visit/<id>`) and its sheet, a section added under a parent, a document added for its holder and the steps of "Scriu eu" open behind their section's lock; a deadline's page, a record's page, a category, a firm and a checklist keep drawing a locked state of their own that names nothing (`.drawnByItsScreen`). A new top-level section, or a document with no holder yet, reveals nothing and opens as it is.
- **Decided (owner):** for a locked category, snoozing („Amână”) stays free, even from the Lock Screen, while closing a deadline („Am rezolvat”, „Gata”) needs Face ID or the passcode, from every way in. One rule (`DeadlineChange`, in `ios/Shared`), read by `IntentEngine.perform` through `BiometricVaultManager.confirmClosing` and by Urmează: a reminder of a locked category gets its own notification category whose „Am rezolvat” opens the app and asks before it acts (`ReminderActions.protectedCategoryIdentifier`; „Amână” keeps working where it is); Siri and Shortcuts continue in the app and ask there; the widget, which can't ask, offers no „Gata” for it and opens the app instead, and a late tap left by an older widget changes nothing and waits in Urmează. A Face ID that isn't given changes nothing: the deadline waits in Urmează, and a reminder opens its page. *Why:* closing a deadline writes into the locked section (its history, a new cycle), while a snooze only moves a reminder.
- **Decided (owner):** with the app lock on, reminders and widgets keep naming the deadline and its section; only a locked category's deadlines use the generic words ("Scadență protejată", "Deschide Dosar cu Șină pentru detalii."). This replaces the line under "The iPhone's search" that tied the iPhone's search to them: while the app lock is on, the iPhone's search shows nothing of the app, as decided under "The iPhone's search and the app lock".

### Ready for App Store Connect
- **Decided (owner):** „Salvează imaginea” stays in a document's share sheet (*Trimite*). iOS asks once for permission to add to Photos, with the app's words: „Ca să salvezi în Poze paginile pe care le trimiți.” (`NSPhotoLibraryAddUsageDescription`). The app never reads Photos, so it asks nothing else of them. **Why:** saving a page to Photos is a plain thing to do with a picture, and the share sheet already offers it; without the words iOS would stop the app the first time.
- **Decided:** whether a build syncs with iCloud is one build setting, `DOSAR_SYNC_SHIPS_IN_RELEASE_BUILDS` (NO until the owner ships sync), read by the code as `PersistenceController.syncShipsInReleaseBuilds` and by the build for the app's background modes: `remote-notification` (iCloud's silent pushes) stays only in a build that syncs (`scripts/background-modes.sh`), so a TestFlight or App Store build doesn't ask to be woken for a sync it doesn't do, and turning sync on brings it back by itself. This replaces the Swift constant as the switch (the 2026-11-10 decision's „one named switch” is now this setting). *Alternatives:* a second Info.plist for Release, two lists of background modes to keep in step; leaving the mode in, which a reviewer can ask about.
- **Decided:** every part that ships (the app, the widgets, the share extension) carries a privacy manifest that tracks nothing, has no tracking domains and lists no collected data, and declares each kind of API Apple asks a reason for that its code uses: the app its own settings (CA92.1), file dates in its own and the App Group's containers (C617.1) and shown in Adaugă (DDA9.1), and the time since start-up for the lock's clock (35F9.1); the widgets and the share extension the App Group's file dates (C617.1), through the share inbox they are compiled with. No part reads disk space or the keyboards. A test checks each manifest against its part's code both ways. **Why no collected data:** Apple counts as collected only what leaves the phone where the developer or the developer's partners (code added to the app: analytics, ads, SDKs) can reach it. The binder goes only to the person's own private iCloud database, which we can't read; a document read with the person's AI provider goes, with their own key, to the provider they chose under their own terms, which is no partner of ours and keeps nothing for us. Guideline 5.1.2(i) still asks that sending to a third-party AI is said and agreed to first: the AI section says so, nothing is sent until the person adds a key and taps „Citește mai bine” or turns on „Citește automat”, which starts off, and an identity document asks every time.
- **Decided:** the export-compliance answer stays `ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption` = NO. A backup's password is turned into a key with CommonCrypto's PBKDF2 and the backup sealed with CryptoKit's AES-GCM, and secret codes with CryptoKit too: only the encryption iOS provides, which Apple's guidance says needs no documentation in App Store Connect. *To confirm (owner):* the yearly self-classification report Apple mentions for exempt encryption.
- **Decided:** the app icons are saved without an alpha channel (they were already fully opaque), as App Store Connect requires of the 1024 pt icon; a test keeps it so.

### Values from outside, and the app's own steps waiting for the whole binder
- **Decided (owner, from the review):** a value read from a backup or another device is held to what the app can work with where it is read, once: a repeat is at most a hundred years in its unit (`RecurrenceInterval.longest(in:)`, which the form's stepper and the counter's projection read too), a counter's step at most ten million, a category's place on the binder within ±2²⁰ (`CategoryOrder`), a reminder's time within the day (`ReminderClock.clamped`, at import and when a reminder fires). A category of one's own has no `singular`, whatever a backup says; a gender a build doesn't know keeps the name.
- **Decided (owner, from the review):** archives get a cap on every file together once unpacked (8 GB, checked by the writer and the reader alike) and refuse files whose bytes overlap, both against the whole directory before a byte is read. This replaces "the 512 MB cap on the whole archive is gone" of 2026-11-10 for what is unpacked (that cap guarded memory; this one guards the phone's storage); the archive itself stays a plain ZIP's 4 GB, which a binder of photos and papers reaches long before 8 GB unpacked, so no household that could have a backup loses it. *Alternative:* no total cap, which lets an archive of a few GB fill the phone when brought in.
- **Decided (owner, from the review):** "Înlocuiește tot" replaces the binder in place: a record in both keeps its copy (`copyID`) with the backup's values; only what the backup doesn't have goes. Deleting everything and making it again gave every record a new copy, which another device's join could keep the old copy of and then lose. The old way stays in the importer, unused (`deleteEverything`), until this is proven on devices.
- **Decided:** a copy from before database version 13 whose device is gone stays a copy; no device names another's, not even once the binder has all arrived. Two devices naming one copy at the same moment would decide differently and each delete the copy the other kept; a name drawn from what a copy holds can differ while an edit travels and is the same for two copies alike in everything. Nothing is lost; the person can delete one copy. *Alternative:* naming them once the binder is whole, which risks losing the record on every device.
- **Decided:** a relaunch in the middle of the first iCloud download still waits for it (the account the binder synced with is kept when sync first opens, so it never stands for a finished download), and a store that syncs is whole only once this launch's first download has finished well. While the share inbox can't be read (the phone locked), what waits is "not known", never "nothing", so the share's notice stays.

## 2026-11-16

### What the copy matrix still showed reading wrong
- **Decided (owner):** a new section is named as one of its category, never the category's plural. Every built-in declares what one section is called and that noun's gender (`CategoryBehavior.singular`, a `SectionSingular`, a required argument: *Mașina*, *Locuința*, *Actul*, *Animalul*, *Fișa medicală*, *Produsul*, *Abonamentul*, *Obiectul*, *Bicicleta*, *Planta*, *Chiriașul*, *Persoana*, *Espressorul*); the name field shows it before anything is typed and a section left unnamed gets it (an empty Garanții section is *Produsul*, no longer *Garanții*). A category of one's own is asked its name as one thing already („Barca, Rulota, Pianul”), so its sections take its name (`TemplateDefinition.sectionName`); no gender is guessed from it, since only a built-in's sections are ever spoken of in a gender (the examples' question); no extra question. The examples' question („ca a ta?” / „ca al tău?”) reads the same declared gender instead of a table of its own. Built-in catalog version 21; `.dosar` carries `singular` in a category's behaviour. *Alternative:* asking a category of one's own for its singular; not done, one more setting for a name already asked as one thing.
- **Decided (owner):** a completion is what its deadline's completion kind says, not what its importance said. Every `CompletionKind` states its record (`CompletionKind.record`, a `CompletionRecord`, with no default: a job, upkeep at home, a check, care, a vaccine, a bill, a tax, a policy, something bought, a renewal, an end only noted), and the history kind a completion writes comes from it (`HistoryKind.forCompleting(_:importance:)`): a rovinietă and the kit are bought (`purchase`), a pet's rabies vaccine is a vaccine, not a check. Its page in Istoric reads the deadline's completion now (`HistoryEntryShape.make(completing:stored:visit:)`): a policy says *Ce acoperă · Asigurătorul · Polița nr.*, a tax *Ce ai plătit · Cui ai plătit*, a renewed document *Cine l-a emis* (agreeing with its paper), upkeep at home *Cine s-a ocupat*, never the vet or the service. Records written before keep the kind they were stored as and read right all the same, so there is no repair. *Alternative:* a `DataRepairs` step rewriting the stored kind of earlier completions; not needed, as nothing but the page and Spotlight's domain reads a completion's kind.
- **Decided (owner):** Acte's empty line names the papers a category's sections keep, as capture files them (`CaptureKindProfile.papersKept(inCategory:)`: a car's talon, policy and ITP report, a product's receipt and warranty certificate, a subscription's contract), never a bill or a visit's invoice (they open from Istoric) or a personal document (a section of its own): „Scanează talonul, polița sau raportul ITP și le găsești aici.”; a category with none says „Scanează un act și îl găsești aici.” Each capture noun declares its paper with its article (`Noun.definite`). The full lists behind „Vezi tot” say their pocket's lines. Date importante's line no longer names a policy for every category.
- **Decided (owner):** what a paper says is on it or in it, as its noun declares (`CaptureKindProfile.Noun.surface`): „Pe factură scrie”, „Pe card scrie”, „În contract scrie”, „În poliță scrie”, „În talon scrie”; the electronic signature is its certificate („În certificatul de semnătură electronică scrie”) and a medical result its *buletin de analize*. The cazier's field is *Ce scrie în cazier*.
- **Decided (owner):** a policy's bonus-malus class is said and kept only when the policy files into a deadline that keeps who it insures (`BlueprintFacts.policyholder`: a car's RCA), never by the deadline's name; the review reads it from its filing plan. A home's policy or a CASCO says nothing about a class.
- **Decided:** *Polița nouă*'s photo says which paper („Cea primită de la asigurător sau de la broker.”), not what is read from it, which its purpose already says. The matrix checks no two sentences on one form open the same way.
- **Decided (owner):** the category-of-one's-own form (`CustomCategoryForm`, its counter's words on `CategorySwitches.Counter`, the rest `CustomCategoryWording`), the photo kept on request (`KeepPhotoChoice`, by `PhotoOnRequest`) and the full lists behind „Vezi tot” (`SectionLists`, `SectionListWording`) are shaped screens, registered with the check and in the matrix. The full list's date question no longer gives an ITP as its example for every category.
- **Decided (owner):** a whole number of lei is counted as Romanian counts it: „1 leu”, „2 lei”, „19 lei”, „20 de lei”, „101 lei”, „1.250 de lei”; with decimals it is „lei” („1,50 lei”, „1,00 lei”). The leu's plural forms are the String Catalog's (`Money.wholeLei`, one/few/other), never written by hand; other currencies keep their code. This replaces the decision below that wrote every amount „lei”.

### Every deadline states what closing it asks; the other shared screens are shaped
- **Decided (owner):** what „Am rezolvat” asks is stated by each deadline, not read off its kind. Every blueprint names its completion (`ObligationBlueprint.completion`, a `CompletionKind`, a required argument with no default), described once (`CompletionKind.shape`), with the paper by its own name and its noun's gender (*Raportul ITP*, *Polița nouă*, *Carnetul de sănătate*, *Procesul-verbal*, *Rezultatele*, *Adeverința de vaccinare*). A deadline of one's own takes it from its importance, as before (`CompletionKind.own`). A blueprint stored before blueprints stated one (a category of one's own made by an earlier version) reads as one of one's own of its kind, which for its periodic service is the same shape; no repair step. The kind is stored by name in the blueprint (`"completion": "itp"`, catalog 20, no database change). The first way back (`CompletionShape.asksByKind`) stays, and a second one reads every deadline as its kind again (`CompletionKind.statedByBlueprints`).
- **Decided:** the matrix's cases. *Garanția se termină* and *Se termină perioada minimă* are only to be known: one tap, as a chore (*acknowledged*), no new document. A renewed contract asks *Contractul nou* and *Se termină pe*. *Vaccin antirabic* and *Vaccin anual* ask the pet's *Carnetul de sănătate*; *Trusă medicală și stingător* asks the price and when the new ones expire, no paper. Descaling and the water filter, and a pet's deworming, are work done at home (*doneAtHome*): the day and the cost, no invoice, and the counter only where the deadline counts on it (descaling every 300 cafele asks *Cafele făcute*; the filter, by time, asks none). A vehicle's work asks the section's counter whatever it repeats by (*Schimb de anvelope* keeps its kilometres). *Alternatives:* the warranty's end asking for an extended warranty's paper; no counter at all at home (descaling would lose what its next one counts from).
- **Decided:** money is written in lei, „1.250 lei”, by the one formatter (`Money.formatted`, `formattedCompact`), as the copy guide says; other currencies keep their code. *Alternative:* „1 leu” for exactly one; not done, an amount reads as a price.
- **Decided:** the other shared screens are shaped screens, registered with the `text-in-shaped-screen` check and in the copy matrix: the deadline form (its own deadline's example now fits any section, „O verificare, o plată, o programare”, instead of „Cazier pentru angajare”), a section page's pockets and „Adaugă…” (Istoric's and Oameni's empty lines by the visit kind), adding a section („Cum vrei să apară în dosar”, with an example from the category's role and section noun), the capture review (each kind's noun and gender, `CaptureKindProfile.noun`, and what its day means, `dayMeaning`: „Unde o pun” for a policy, „ITP-ul e valabil până pe …”) and a record's page in Istoric (`HistoryEntryShape`: a bill, a purchase or a note asks no stars; a chore done with *Gata* asks none unless someone did it; a vet's visit names the vet; a deletion speaks of parts only for a vehicle). *Alternative:* a declared example name per category, a new stored value; not done.
- **Decided:** the String Catalogs are committed in the form a build in Xcode writes, checked against the owner's Xcode build of `main` (its output reproduced byte for byte by `--sync` from the same build's strings): Xcode's own sync, the entries in Finder's order in English, nothing only a `#Preview` uses, no newline at the end. A release build found fewer strings than a development build (the gallery's sample words), so each rewrote the other's catalog: the gallery's words are now verbatim, and the release job runs the check. *Alternative:* marking the gallery's words as managed by hand, which says something untrue about them.

### A visit's form comes from its kind
- **Decided (owner):** a visit's form comes from its category's kind, never from a default. Every category states what its visits are (`CategoryBehavior.visit`, a `VisitKind`, with no default value: the compiler refuses a built-in that forgets, and `TemplateDefinition` takes a behaviour with no default either), and „no visits” is a kind of its own. Each kind's description (`VisitShape`, one place, as `CompletionShape` is for „Gata”) says the add menu's title, the form's sections, fields and words, the paper it asks for, whether there is a checklist (only where the category lists parts), the counter, who did it and the trade, what saving needs, the button and the toast. The kinds, from the owner's table: *service* (a car, a bike: the invoice, the parts with brand and price, the kilometres, the firm, the total, as before); *job* (a home: what was done, who did it, the cost, the invoice); *repair* (something bought: what was fixed, where, the cost, whether it was under warranty); *upkeep* (an espresso machine: what was done, the cost if any); *doctor* (the day, the doctor or clinic, what for, the papers: rețeta, analizele, biletul, an optional cost; it saves with its day alone, no parts, no brand, no „Bifează ce s-a făcut”); *vet* (the day, the vet, what was done from the pet's vaccines, deparazitări and treatments, the papers, an optional cost); *none* (subscriptions, people, documents, tenancy: no visit offered anywhere, not in the section page's „Adaugă…” menu, not in „Scriu eu”, not for capture's invoice; an intervention on a line belongs to the home); and plants take a dated note in Istoric, „Adaugă o notă” (*note*), through the one note path (`HistoryNoteSheet`, `HistoryActions.addNote`: a record of kind note, its first line its title). A category of one's own has visits only with its switch on, renamed „Are revizii sau lucrări”.
- **Decided (owner):** a shared screen holds no words of its own. Every word on the visit form comes from its kind's description or the words every kind shares (`VisitFormWording`); the architecture check refuses a text literal in a registered list of shaped screens (`text-in-shaped-screen`, the visit form first; the next pull request adds the others), with fixtures and no exceptions.
- **Decided (owner):** a copy matrix. `CopyMatrixTests` writes `docs/COPY_MATRIX.md` from the descriptions (the visit form, and „Gata”/„Am rezolvat” from `CompletionShape`), for every built-in and one category of one's own per combination of its switches, and fails when the committed file is out of date; the same test keeps vehicle words („km”, „kilometraj”, „piese”, „marcă”, „ulei”, „bord”) in a vehicle's kind, every save requirement within the form's own fields and no empty checklist. AGENTS.md: a new shared screen's varying parts are a declared kind with an entry in the matrix, and reviews walk every category through it.
- **Decided:** a category of one's own with „Are revizii sau lucrări” on gets a *service* when it is counted by kilometres or engine hours (a machine looked after by use, like a car: the form asks the counter and the parts), and a *job* when it goes by time (a piano, a boiler: someone comes to do it). One rule, `CategorySwitches.visit(for:)`; changing only how often leaves a stated kind as it is. A category an earlier version made with the switch on stored no kind: it gets its switch's kind by itself at launch and after every import (`OwnCategoryVisitsStep`), so its sections keep logging visits. *Alternatives:* a job for every one of one's own (a boat by engine hours would lose its counter), or a choice of kind in the form (one more question).
- **Decided:** *Altele* states *job* (it was the plain „Am făcut o lucrare” before); the home keeps its title, „Am chemat un meseriaș”, which is the job kind's. *Alternative:* „Am făcut o lucrare” as the job's title, which reads as done by oneself.
- **Decided:** a repair says whether it was under warranty from the product's own warranty end (`FieldRole.warrantyUntil`), under the date („Era în garanție, până pe …” / „Garanția se terminase pe …”), never a new stored value. *Alternative:* a „În garanție” switch kept on the record, a change to the database.
- **Decided:** the espresso machine's upkeep asks no paper and no firm, as the table says; capture's invoice for one is filed as a document by the review. A doctor's or a vet's paper is kept as a report and known again only as the same document (another photo of an invoice is known by its number, day and total). A captured invoice opens the visit form only where the section's kind keeps a paper (`CaptureKindProfile.opensVisit(into:)`), and the form is the section's kind's. *Alternative:* the espresso's upkeep with an optional invoice and firm.
- **Decided:** a visit's kind is stored as its name (`"visit": "doctor"` in a category's behaviour and in `.dosar`); the earlier object of words reads as the kind its trade names, a missing one as none, a newer one as a job. The built-in catalog is version 19. The one form of before, the same for every category, is kept switched off as the way back (`VisitShape.formsByKind`).

### A category of one's own: one box is its grouping, and it can belong to a home, a car or a person
- **Decided (owner):** one rule for one box. A category of one's own's *O cutie pentru toate* is the category's grouping (`CategorySwitches.grouping`, `CategoryTemplate.chosenGrouping`), the same choice a box's menu, the category's page and Setări › Ecranul Dosar make, read and written both ways: no second way to one box. Its subtitle says *Ca la plante: toate stau la un loc.* A category of one's own is always a box per section; one an earlier version made with *O cutie pentru toate* (a box of the whole category's, as Garanții's) gets a box per section, grouped, which looks the same (the same one box, opening the same page, in the same place), by itself at launch and after every import (`OwnCategoryBoxesStep`, runs by itself as it changes nothing anyone sees); a grouping already chosen stays. Only a box the switches wrote moves: a category of one's own that behaves like Garanții (its own list, words or part, brought by a backup) keeps its box, and its *Câte o cutie pentru fiecare* gives it a box each. This replaces the earlier *Câte o cutie pentru fiecare* / *O cutie pentru toate* as a box (2026-11-03) and closes what "One box for several sections" left open. *Differences:* the one box now waits for a section before it shows (it already folded when empty), and the add box names it among the others rather than first; Setări's lock list shows it once it holds a section. *Alternatives:* the switch writes the category's default grouping (`defaultGrouping`), which a box's menu would then override, so the form would stop meaning what it says; or keep two kinds of one box.
- **Decided (owner):** *Ține de…* for a category of one's own: *Nimic*, *O locuință*, *O mașină*, *O persoană*, with one line under it („Fiecare apare și pe pagina celei de care ține și se adaugă și de acolo.”). It is what plants say (`mayBelongTo` with `goesUnderTheOnlyOne`), for any part that holds sections (`CategoryBehavior.Role.parents`: home, a car's new part `vehicle` (catalog 18), a person), offered from the parts the binder's categories play (`CategorySwitches.belongingChoices`), never a category's name. Its sections, filed under a car, are listed on that car's page in a pocket named after the category (*Anvelope*), added from there (*Adaugă în „Anvelope”*, in the page's *Adaugă…* menu until it holds one), moved with *Mută la…* (to another car, or *În „Anvelope”, fără mașină*) and still in their own box and page; with exactly one car a new one goes under it (the add form shows *Mașina* chosen). One path for every part: the page's pockets are what its category lists plus one per category that belongs to the part it plays and has no pocket of the catalog's (`PagePocket`, `CategoryBehavior.hasPocketOfItsOwn`), and the words of a part are one place (`BelongingWording`); built-in plants and subscriptions keep their catalog pockets. The suggestion to move sections standing on their own under the only one covers these too, in the part's words (*Ce ține de altă secțiune*).
- **Decided:** choosing a part when it said *Nimic* also chooses *O cutie pentru toate*, as plants start, so a section filed under a car still shows on the binder; the person can change it right under. *Alternative:* leave the box as it was, and a section filed under a car shows only on the car's page.
- **Decided:** changing *Ține de…* to *Nimic* or another part brings back on their own the sections filed under one that no longer fits, in the same step as the change and its *Anulează* (`TemplateStore.editCustom`), so nothing is left where no page lists it. *Alternative:* leave them filed and keep the pocket while it holds them, a second rule for when a pocket shows.
- **Decided:** a car plays a part of its own (`CategoryBehavior.Role.vehicle`) so "a car" is data like "a home"; an older build reads it as no part. Bikes don't play it.

### One meaning per word
- **Decided (owner):** every word the person meets means one thing, app-wide: the app, the widgets, the share sheet, notifications, Siri and Shortcuts, VoiceOver's labels and hints, onboarding, Settings and the copy guide. *De verificat* is only the share inbox; the repair list is *De corectat* („Nimic de corectat”, „Un lucru de corectat”, „3 lucruri de corectat”; its prompt „Am găsit ceva de corectat”). The locks list categories, so it is *Categorii protejate*, and „secțiune” stays for one section (a car, a home). *Gata* means only done: a sheet or a page closes with *Închide*. *Anulează* means only taking back the step just taken (the undo toast): a sheet or a flow is left with *Renunță*, and „Renunți la modificări?” stays. Backups are *copie de siguranță* everywhere, the copy guide's „copie de rezervă” included. A glossary at the top of docs/COPY_GUIDE.md says each word, what it means, where it is used and what not to say. **Why:** a flow review found the same word doing two jobs on neighbouring screens (*De verificat* in Adaugă and in Settings; *Gata* both completing a chore and closing Settings; *Anulează* both cancelling a sheet and taking back a step).
- **Decided:** the share sheet's button, once something has arrived (or it couldn't be), is *Închide*, not *Gata*: the share is already made when the confirmation shows, so the button only closes the sheet, and *Gata* keeps its one meaning (a deadline done). While it copies it says *Renunță*. Both words come from `ShareConfirmation.Action.title`. *Alternative:* keep *Gata* as the end of a completed share; it would be the one *Gata* that closes nothing of the person's, and it shows after a failure too.
- **Decided:** an editor that keeps what was typed (a section's, a document's, a person's or firm's *Editează*, a row under *Date importante*, a bill line) confirms with *Salvează*, as the copy guide's "verbs on buttons, naming the result" asks and as a category's editor already did. A sheet that only shows or whose choices are kept as they are made (Setări, the full-screen pages, *Când te anunț*) closes with *Închide*. *Alternative:* *Închide* for the editors too, which would hide that they save.
- **Decided:** *Verifică* stays only where it asks the person to look again at a value read off a paper: the review's title *Verifică actul*, „Verifică ce e marcat” and the mark beside a value. A readiness check worth a look is read by VoiceOver as *Merită o privire* (it said *De verificat*); a trip with nothing to check is „Nimic de pregătit încă”; the review's other notices are behind „Mai sunt 2 observații” (opened from the share inbox, „Mai sunt 2 lucruri de verificat” read like more items waiting there). In an error's next step the plain verb stays („Verifică dacă ai copiat-o întreagă”). Errors about a backup say „copia de siguranță”, never „arhiva”, which is a document put away (*Mută în arhivă*). In *Adaugă o secțiune*, the other kinds are *Alte categorii*.
- **Decided:** a dialog never gets the system's own cancel button, which iOS words „Anulează”: every one names its cancel (*Renunță*, or *Păstrează* before a deletion). A snooze is stopped with *Nu mai amâna*. Face ID's prompt cancels with *Renunță*. **Not changed, and why:** the system's own screens (the camera, the photo picker, the document scanner, the share sheet's own chrome) say „Anulează” in Romanian; the app can't word them.
- **Decided (owner):** *Scriu eu* in Adaugă opens only what is typed in: a deadline or a visit, for the section chosen. Its *Un act* card, which opened capture for a photo, is gone from it (kept switched off, `ManualAddFlow.offersDocumentPhoto`): a paper is added with a photo from Adaugă's three other ways in, or from the section's own *Adaugă un act*, which already takes the photo for that section. **Why:** the flow matches the label, Adaugă reads as three ways with a paper and one without, and adding a section's paper keeps one path. *Alternative:* rename the card to what it opened (*Alege secțiunea*), which keeps a second way to photograph a section's paper and a label that says nothing about typing.
- **Decided (owner):** Urmează's left swipe *Editează* is gone: it only opened the page, which a tap already does. Completing from Urmează reads and does what the page's button does for that kind, through one entry (`CompletionEntry.start`, the page and the row alike): a chore's *Gata* is done at once with its toast; any other kind's *Am rezolvat* (in a menu and as VoiceOver's action *Am rezolvat…*, the ellipsis because it asks before anything is done) opens the same sheet (`completionSheet`, `ObligationCompleteSheet`). This replaces the quick way in Urmează's swipe and menu for every kind, decided with "What „Am rezolvat” asks is the deadline's kind's": only outside the app (reminders, the widget, Siri) does every kind still complete in one step. *Alternative:* keep the quick completion in Urmează for every kind, as before; then the same deadline would close differently from its page and from the list.
- **Decided:** the easy part is checked, not remembered: the architecture check refuses a literal „Anulează” outside the undo toast (`anuleaza-outside-undo`), „Gata” outside the completion wording and the widget (`gata-outside-done`), and „De verificat” outside the share inbox (`de-verificat-outside-inbox`), each with a failing and a passing fixture; `OneMeaningPerWordTests` pins what each home says and that both screens start completing the same way. Stored data, keys and identifiers are unchanged: only text.

### A section's page shows what it holds, and adds from where you are
- **Decided (owner):** from a flow and clutter review. A pocket with nothing in it isn't drawn as a card: every empty pocket folds into one *Adaugă…* row at the end of the pockets, a menu naming what can be added there (*Adaugă un termen*, *Adaugă un act*, *Am fost la service*, *Adaugă un chiriaș*, *Adaugă o plantă*, *Adaugă o persoană*, *Adaugă un rând*), each item under the pocket it goes in and opening its own flow over the page, for this section. A pocket takes its usual place as soon as it holds something; the one global order stays. *Bani* adds nothing itself and shows only once there is money. This replaces SPEC 2.3's "every pocket always appears". **Why:** a new home showed about eight empty cards and a car five, and an empty *Bani* had no action at all.
- **Decided:** one rule for every page (`SectionPageLayout`, read from `PocketContents`), the section's page and a person's document page alike, so no pocket decides by itself whether it shows. The menu's items are the pockets' own additions (`PocketAddition`): a pocket's button under its rows and the menu say and do the same. A pocket of sections that have boxes of their own (plants on a home) is still not offered while the household keeps none, as decided for plants. Adding a first row under *Date importante* from the menu shows the pocket in its place with the row open; left empty, it goes back into the menu. The earlier page is kept switched off (`SectionPageLayout.foldsEmptyPockets`) until the owner has used the new one. *Alternatives:* one small row per empty pocket, which keeps most of the clutter; the menu in the toolbar, where a new section's first step would be hidden.
- **Decided (owner):** add from where you are. The Acte pocket's *Adaugă un act* (*Scanează un document* beside a person's own documents) takes a photo, a picture or a file through capture for this section (`photoFirstCapture(…assetID:)`), instead of switching to the Adaugă tab and asking *Unde îl pun* again. Urmează's *Adaugă un termen* opens the one deadline form (`DeadlineForm`) for the section; *Vezi tot* shows when there are more than three or some are paused. The list behind it no longer offers the usual deadlines as rows of their own (*Poți adăuga*, each with its own date sheet, `FirstDateSheet`): they are in the form, so one job has one path, as "One form for a new deadline" said. The rows and the sheet are kept switched off (`ObligationListScreen.offersSuggestionRows`).
- **Decided (owner):** each paper is shown once. A paper filed with a record (a visit's invoice, a bill, an inspection's report) is that record's and opens from it in Istoric, not again in Acte, and doesn't count there (`SectionPapers`, `Attachment.isItsRecordsPaper`, the one rule for the pocket, *Vezi tot* and their counts). A view rule only: the paper stays filed under the section as before. **Decided:** a paper valid until a day (`validUntil`: a policy, a document renewed with *Am rezolvat*, which files its new paper with the completion's record) stays in Acte too, since it stands for the section. *Alternative:* every paper with a record leaves Acte, strictly; a renewed RCA policy would then be found only under its completion in Istoric.
- **Decided (owner, option a):** adding a section never stops on dates. A deadline starts ticked only when its category suggests it and its first date is worked out (from a field, an interval after today, its next fixed date); one whose date must be asked (a car's ITP, RCA and rovinietă) starts unticked, with one line under the list: „Pe cele care cer o dată le poți adăuga și mai târziu, din pagina secțiunii sau dintr-o poză a actului.” One rule for every category, by the blueprint's `firstDue` (`PlannedObligation.startsSelected`), read by the add flow, a typed-in document and the examples. A row the person ticks still asks its date; a date typed later in the essentials ticks the row it works out.
- **Decided (owner):** the Acte box adds a document photo first. One *Adaugă un act*, under the groups and in the empty state: capture reads the type, names whose it is and makes the person when they aren't in the binder yet; *Scriu eu* asks *Al cui e?* first (one of the household's people, or *Altcineva* with a name, made with the document in one step: `PersonalDocumentFiling.save(_:holder:)`). Each person's card starts with their own row, which opens their page; the repeated *Vezi tot despre …*, *Adaugă un act* per person and *Adaugă o persoană* are gone. *Alternative:* keep a header with the person's name and count above each card; the name would show twice.
- **Decided:** a pocket's button is a `DSSecondaryButton` (`PocketAddButton`), Istoric's visit button too: a section's page has no one main action. The `main-action-in-scroll` check now also refuses a `DSPrimaryButton` inside a pocket's card (`DSPocketCard`), wherever it is written, with a failing fixture of its own (the self-test reads every `<rule>.fail-<what>.swift`); no exceptions. **Not checked, and why:** a primary button in a struct that some `ScrollView` names. Following structs through the braces flags two views today: *Trimite* right under a paper's picture (`DocumentShareButton`, that page's main action, rightly there) and the „Gata” of *Date importante*'s inline editor (an editor inside its card, not the page's end); braces can't tell the top of a page from its end.

### The capture review asks less and says one thing at a time
- **Decided (owner):** from a flow and clutter review. Closing the review asks *Renunți la modificări?* only once the person changed something the reading proposed (a value typed, chosen, added or cleared, the kind, the title, the place, a new section's name, a reminder, direct debit, *E altă factură*, the photo kept, a page's framing), by one rule (`CaptureReview.hasPersonsChanges`); a review left as read closes at once, and a reading again is not a change of theirs. **Why:** it asked every time, even with nothing changed, and from De verificat it implied a loss that doesn't happen.
- **Decided:** from De verificat the close button says *Mai târziu*, and the question, when there is one, says „Ce ai schimbat nu se salvează. Actul rămâne în De verificat.” (`dsDiscardGuard(hasChanges:message:)`, `DSCancelButton(_:)`). *Alternative:* keep *Anulează* and say it only in the question; not taken, because with nothing changed there is no question, and *Anulează* beside *Șterge* reads as throwing it away.
- **Decided (owner):** what needs confirming most comes first, in one card: what it is, *Unde îl pun* and *Când te anunț* (with *Schimbă ziua*, which scrolls to the date the reminder follows). Under it one notice at a time, by one rule (`ReviewNotice.importance`): the back being read, the question about sending the photo, a bill already in the binder, the same photo, values to check, the AI provider's failed reading, the offer to add the back, a photo hard to read, a long PDF read in part; the others behind *Mai sunt 2 lucruri de verificat*, which shows them all. *Citește mai bine cu …* (or why a bank card has none) moved under the values, where a wrong value is seen. *Alternative:* the notices stay at the top in a list that collapses; not taken, as one rule for which comes first is what keeps the top calm.
- **Decided (owner):** *Unde îl pun* lists first what fits the document (`CaptureDestinations`): its category (a policy's by its cover) and routing's, a bill's subscriptions and homes, a personal document's people (never each person's documents one by one), and always the place shown; then *Secțiune nouă în „…”* for the category it fits and *Altă secțiune…*, with every section and *Secțiune nouă* from the catalog. A document that fits nothing in particular lists everything at once. A new section is made only by *Adaugă o secțiune* (`AddSectionFlow` with an `AddSectionRequest`), and the document goes into it; the picker closes once that flow's sheet has gone. The earlier name-only *Secțiune nouă* (every category, hidden ones too) is switched off (`CaptureDestinations.makesSectionFromAName`), kept as the way back. A section made this way is saved by its own flow, before the review saves, so it stays if the review is then closed. The reading's own proposal of a new section (a car from its talon, a new subscription, *Persoană nouă* named after the holder) is still made when the review saves, as before.
- **Decided (owner):** an item from De verificat can be taken out from its review: *Șterge din De verificat* in the toolbar, with the same toast and *Anulează* as *Șterge* on its row, by one path (`IntakeInbox.dismissal`, `CaptureFlowResult.removedFromInbox`). No question first: *Anulează* brings it back.
- **Decided (owner):** *Închide* while capture reads, which stops the reading and lets go of the pages (`CaptureSession`).
- **Decided (owner):** a visit ends once, as every save: the form closes with „Vizita e în istoric.” and *Anulează*, from capture, *Scriu eu* and Istoric alike (`VisitEnding.toast`; from Istoric on the shell's host). What changed is on the visit's entry. The *Ce s-a schimbat* screen with its *Gata* and *Anulează vizita* is switched off (`VisitEnding.showsWhatChanged`), kept as the way back.
- **Decided:** the empty binder's *Adaugă primul lucru* is pinned to the bottom edge with *Încarcă exemple* under it, and a document page's *Adaugă poza actului* is one card the whole of which is the action (`DSActionCard`): neither is a main button inside scrolling content any more. The `main-action-in-scroll` check reads one struct at a time, so a main button in a struct of its own placed inside a scroll view still passes it.

### Code never names a frozen database version
- **Decided:** outside `Models/Schema/` code names a model by its alias and the schema as `CurrentSchema`, never `DosarSchemaV<N>`; the `frozen-schema-version` check refuses `DosarSchemaV<N>.` and `extension DosarSchemaV<N>` everywhere else, with no exceptions (the services that named the current version use the aliases now). **Why:** a deadline's cycle reminders extended version 14's model by name and reached only the frozen copy once version 15 came. The next version changes the aliases and `CurrentSchema` in one file.

### Settings is a short list of pages
- **Decided (owner):** Settings stacked twelve sections in one list, with long footers, a switch for every category's lock and the AI model picker in plain view. It becomes a short list of rows, each opening a page and saying in a few words how it stands: *Notificări* („Pornite, la 09:00”), *Protecție* („Face ID pentru 2 categorii”), *Ecranul Dosar* („Toate categoriile se văd”), *iCloud și copii* („Totul e sincronizat”, else „Doar pe telefon”), *Citire cu AI* („Google Gemini”, else „Oprită, citesc pe telefon”), *Date de verificat* („Nimic de verificat”; the name stays for now), *Exemple* (only while examples are in the binder) and *Despre* (the version). Every setting is still there, on its page.
- **Decided:** the pages are the same sections the list stacked, a few to a page, so the two can't drift: *Protecție* holds the locks and the iPhone's search; *Ecranul Dosar* which categories show, one box or one each, the boxes' first order and *Categoriile tale*; *iCloud și copii* sync, backups and the space documents take. The AI model sits under *Avansat*, open by itself for a compatible service, which names its model, or when a model was chosen. The words of each row are one pure place (`SettingsStatus`), tested.
- **Decided:** a link opens its page over the list (`SettingsPage(section)`, `SettingsPage.path(for:)`), the repair prompt's *Arată-mi* included; a new link, `dosarcusina://settings/ai`. The earlier list stays, switched off (`SettingsScreen.usesPages`, `SettingsListScreen`, with its `SettingsLanding`), as the way back until the owner has used the pages.
- *Alternatives:* a status as the row's trailing value, which truncates on a phone in Romanian; *Spațiu ocupat* in *Despre*, rejected because it is about what the binder keeps, beside backups.

### Examples are removed where they are
- **Decided (owner):** while examples are in the binder, its end says what they are („Ce e marcat „Exemplu” e doar ca să vezi cum arată un dosar plin.”) and offers *Șterge exemplele*. Urmează's footnote pointing to Setări › Exemple is gone; each example row keeps its *Exemplu* mark.
- **Decided:** one step wherever it is offered (`ExampleRemoval.remove`, over `ExampleDataService.removeExamples`), undoable from its toast, so neither place asks first any more (Settings asked, then offered the undo too). The words that sent people to Settings (the first run's footnote, the toast after loading, onboarding) now point to the end of the binder.
- *Alternative:* a row at the top of the grid; rejected as louder than examples deserve.

### Empty boxes fold into the add box, which names them
- **Decided (owner):** Acte, Garanții and Abonamente showed *Gol deocamdată* and Agendă *Nimeni încă* from the first section. A category with nothing in it has no box, and Agendă shows once it holds someone; they fold into the add box, which names what can be added that has no box yet, so a new person still sees the app's range: „Adaugă o secțiune” · „Acte, Garanții, Abonamente, Locuințe…”. A category's box appears in its place as soon as it holds something, next to its neighbours as any new box does.
- **Decided:** one rule for what a box holds (`BinderLayout.holds`: its own sections, and for the box of personal documents the household's people, who live inside it, so a person added before any document still has a way in), read by the grid and by the box's line. What the add box names comes from the one list of offers (`SectionOffers.inOrder`, through `BinderLayout.addBoxNames`): the folded boxes first, in the binder's order, then the rest as *Adaugă o secțiune* offers them, four at most, then „…”; never a hidden category, never one whose sections live inside another. With nothing left to name it says only „Adaugă o secțiune”.
- **Decided:** a category of one's own that keeps *O cutie pentru toate* folds the same way when empty; that switch no longer means a box that stays when empty.
- **Left open:** a contact is added only from a section (*Contacte*, a visit) while Agendă is folded; the add box doesn't offer one. *Alternative, for the owner:* keep Agendă always, or offer *Un contact* in *Adaugă o secțiune*.

### Grouping is offered where the boxes are arranged; a menu of one shows its item
- **Decided (owner):** one box or one each is offered in a box's press-and-hold menu (and VoiceOver's actions) and mirrored in Setări › Ecranul Dosar; the category's page no longer offers it, so its *Mai multe* is gone. This replaces "on the category's page (*Mai multe*)" in "One box for several sections, the person's choice".
- **Decided:** a page's extra actions follow one design-system rule (`DSMoreActionsToolbar`, `DSMoreActionsLayout`): none shows nothing, one shows as itself with its words, two or more share *Mai multe*. The document page's *Mută la…* now shows as itself. The section page is changed by another branch at the same time and is left to adopt it.

### Suggestions aren't repairs
- **Decided (owner):** this changes "The repair list"'s one calm prompt. The repair list is for data an earlier version saved wrongly. A step whose findings are suggestions (nothing wrong; the app can now do better with what is there) says so as data (`DataRepairStep.isSuggestion`); today only plants that can stand in the household's only home (`SectionsIntoTheirHomeStep`). Its findings are never in the repair list, its count or its prompt: they wait as one calm note in Urmează › *Din timp* (`AttentionModel.suggestionNote`, the one place for calm notes, after the bills): *O sugestie pentru dosar* · *Ce stă într-o locuință. Nu schimb nimic fără tine.*, which opens *Sugestii*: the same rows, with the step's own verb (`DataRepairStep.applyTitle`: *Mută*), *Lasă așa* and *Toate*. The note names nothing a locked section wouldn't. The step keeps its id and the answers kept by it, so nothing answered before is asked again.
- **Decided:** the launch prompt stays for corrections only, by the rule it had: once per new finding, only with no sheet, lock or onboarding in front. **Why:** a correction is about data the person relies on now (a deadline's day, a counter), and it comes rarely, after an upgrade or an import; Urmează may not be opened for days. *Alternative, for the owner:* no alert at all, corrections too as a calm row in Urmează or a count on the Settings button.

### A deadline's page: one "Editează", the section's name a link, last time apart from the policy
- **Decided (owner):** from a flow and clutter review: the deadline's page spread one idea over four sheets that saved four ways (*Schimbă data* with *Mută termenul*, *Schimbă când te anunț* saving each tap then *Gata*, *Ora amintirii* with *Salvează ora*, *Schimbă cum se repetă* with *Salvează*), and a deadline of one's own couldn't be renamed: a typo meant deleting it and adding it again. Now one *Editează*, in the toolbar's place (and as *Editează termenul* in the schedule card), opens one sheet (`DeadlineEditSheet`): the name and *Cât de important e* (only a deadline of one's own; a category's keeps its blueprint's name and kind), the date, *Se repetă* (`DSRepeatField`), the reminders (the leads, *Altă zi…*, the follow-up, *Revino la cele obișnuite*) and *Ora amintirii* (`DSReminderTimeField`). One pinned *Salvează* saves it all as one step with one *Anulează* (`ObligationActions.edit`, inside one `UnitOfWork.performUndoable`), laying only what was changed in the sheet over the deadline as it is then (`ThreeWay`, from what the sheet opened with, `DeadlineEdit`); closing asks first only after a real change. Nothing stored changes.
- **Decided (owner):** the page says how it repeats and reminds in one line, in a card of its own (*Cum se repetă și când te anunț*): „La fiecare 3 zile, de la data la care l-ai făcut · Îți amintesc cu o zi înainte, la 07:30”, or „… la ora obișnuită (09:00)” (`ReminderWording.schedule`, built from the same words as the sentences, `RepeatWording.phrase` and the leads' phrase).
- **Decided (owner):** the section's name in the header opens the section (`AppRoute.asset`, `ObligationScreen.sectionRoute`); not for the whole household, and not while the section is locked.
- **Decided (owner):** *Data trecută* holds only the last time it was done (its day, cost, note and who did it); the deadline's own policy (number, claims phone, the policyholder's class) and the documents on file are a card of their own, *Polița*, or *Acte în dosar* when it has only documents (`DeadlinePageFacts`). Each shows only when it has something, and a separator stands only between two rows: the claims phone or the documents no longer open with one.
- **Decided (owner):** the deadline form keeps *Ora amintirii*, collapsed by default to one row („Ora obișnuită (09:00)”, *Schimbă*), opening to the same two choices (`DSReminderTimeField(startsCollapsed:)`); a time of its own opens it from the start. *Editează* uses it the same way.
- **Decided:** two new actions, one path each: `ObligationActions.rename` and `setImportance` (the importance is the kind, through `DeadlineChoice.Importance`), each undoable and laid over what is stored (`ThreeWay`), and refused for a category's deadline (`Obligation.isPersonsOwn`: no blueprint). A new importance gives the deadline the new kind's consequence and grace, which the person can't set, and the new kind's leads and follow-up only while they are still the old kind's own; leads the person chose stay (`DeadlineChoice.reminders(_:from:to:)`, the one rule, read by the action and by the sheet while it is open). *Alternative:* the new kind's reminders whole; rejected because it would drop leads the person chose. A deadline of one's own whose kind isn't one of the four answers (from an older backup) isn't offered the choice and keeps its kind.
- **Decided:** what *Editează* changes, a toast says with *Anulează*: „Am salvat „Udat plantele”.”. It is offered while the deadline runs or is paused, not once it is done or archived, and not while its section is locked. *Amână* and *Am rezolvat* / *Gata* stay as they are.
- **Decided:** the way back is a switch (`ObligationPage.editsInOneSheet`): off, the page shows the four earlier ways again (`RescheduleSheet`, `ObligationRepeatSection`, `ReminderSettingsSheet` with `ReminderTimePage`), kept, not removed, until the owner agrees.

### One box for several sections, the person's choice
- **Decided (owner):** once a category whose sections each have a box has two or more on the binder, the person can group them into one box ("Mașini"), which opens to the category's page listing them, and split them again any time. It is per category (three cars in one box, two flats in a box each), and it is the household's choice: it syncs and travels in backups like a category's place and whether it shows. The grouped box shows how many it holds and the most pressing deadline among them: *3 mașini · ITP · în 5 zile*.
- **Decided:** the choice is stored on the category (`CategoryTemplate.boxGroupingRaw`, database version 15, a lightweight stage) and counts as the person's change of a built-in, beside its order and visibility (`ChangeTimes.personsOwnChoices`), so a newer catalog never turns it back and the newer choice wins between two phones. None stored means the catalog's (`CategoryBehavior.defaultGrouping`); a value from a newer build is kept as written and read as none. *Alternative:* keep it on the device with the box order; rejected because the owner wants it to follow the household, and the box order is the only part of the layout kept per device.
- **Decided:** one rule for what is offered (`BoxGrouping.offer`): grouping from two sections of a category whose sections have boxes of their own; splitting always, even with one left, so there is always a way back. A category with one box (Acte, Garanții, Abonamente) or none (tenants) has no choice. It is offered in a box's press-and-hold menu and VoiceOver's actions, on the category's page (*Mai multe*), and in Setări › Pe ecranul Dosar, which is where the binder is arranged; the words are the same everywhere (*Grupează într-o singură cutie*, *Arată fiecare separat*). The one box takes the place of the first of its sections in the order, and the grid moves with its spring.
- **Decided:** the grouped box's line is the most pressing deadline of all its sections and what is filed under them, ranked as Urmează ranks them (`BoxStatus.make(forAssets:)`), which is the worst of the lines their own boxes would show. It counts them in the category's own words (`CategoryBehavior.sectionNoun`: mașini, locuințe, animale, biciclete, plante, espressoare, with the String Catalog's plural forms); a category without words of its own counts *secțiuni*. A grouped category shows no box while it has no section.
- **Left open:** a category of one's own still answers *O cutie pentru toate* as its box (`CategorySwitches.boxes`, `.wholeCategory`: a box that stays even when empty, as Garanții's). It now sits beside the grouping choice, two ways to one box for such a category. *Alternative, for the owner:* the switch writes the category's default grouping instead, and *O cutie pentru toate* becomes the grouping with no empty box; existing categories would keep theirs.

### Plants share a box and may stand in a home
- **Decided (owner):** plants start as one *Plante* box, not one per plant; cars and homes keep a box each. The catalog says where each starts (`defaultGrouping`), and the person's choice wins.
- **Decided (owner):** a plant may stand in a home or on its own. In a home it is listed on the home's page (a *Plante* pocket, like tenants and subscriptions) and is still in the *Plante* box; *Unde stă* stays its room. With exactly one home, a new plant goes into it by itself and the add form shows it chosen (*Locuința*, which can be changed to another home or *Niciuna*); with several or none it stands on its own. "Mută la…" moves it between homes and back on its own (*În „Plante”, fără locuință*).
- **Decided:** "may stand in a home" is data any category can say (`CategoryBehavior.mayBelongTo`, a part such as `home`, and `goesUnderTheOnlyOne`), read by "Mută la…" (`AssetActions.parentKeys`), the add flow (`AssetActions.automaticParent`, `possibleParents`) and the repair. A subscription says `mayBelongTo: home` too (it already could), so "Mută la…" no longer names built-in categories, and a category of one's own that plays a home's part is offered as well. A home's *Plante* pocket shows once the household keeps plants (the category is on the binder) or the home holds one, so it doesn't sit empty on every home's page.
- **Decided:** plants kept before stand on their own. A repair step at the end of the list (`SectionsIntoTheirHomeStep`) offers each to move into the household's only home, asking first; with several homes it only says to move them with "Mută la…"; examples are left as they are. Built-in catalog 17.
- **Left open:** a category of one's own has no switch for "may stand in a home": its sections would need a pocket of their own on a home's page, named after it, and today pockets are a fixed list. *Alternatives:* a generic pocket on a home's page, one card per such category with its name; or keep it to built-ins.

### A deadline repeats every so many days, weeks, months or years
- **Decided (owner):** from TestFlight feedback (watering plants every 3 days), a deadline can repeat every N days, weeks, months or years, counted from its due day or from the day it was done. The engine already did; the form offered only once, monthly and yearly.
- **Decided:** one control for how a deadline repeats (`DSRepeatField`, in the design system gallery), over one plain value (`RepeatChoice`, a view of an interval rule or none): *Se repetă* with *Nu se repetă*, *Zilnic*, *Săptămânal*, *Lunar*, *Anual*; a stepper that says how many with the String Catalog's plurals („În fiecare zi”, „La fiecare 3 zile”, „La fiecare 20 de zile”); and *De la data termenului* or *De la data la care l-ai făcut*, each with what it means for the next one („Următorul e la 3 zile după ce îl bifezi.”). Words in one place (`RepeatWording`). It is used by the deadline form for a deadline of the person's own and on the deadline's page (*Cum se repetă* › *Schimbă cum se repetă*, a sheet saved with *Salvează* as one undoable step through `ObligationActions.setRecurrence`, laid over the rule as it is then by `ThreeWay`). The due day stays; the new rule counts from the next completion. *Alternative:* save each tap at once, as the reminder settings do; rejected because a stepper would make an undo step per tap.
- **Decided:** a new repeat of days or weeks counts from when it was done (chores: a late one doesn't leave the next already late), of months or years from its due day (payments, as before). The person sees and can change both.
- **Decided:** the built-in deadlines keep their rules; the person may change a deadline's repeat on its page. A rule on fixed dates, by a counter or by law (ITP) is shown in words only and not offered in the control: its category decides it (`CategorySwitches` for a category of one's own).
- **Decided:** the earlier three choices (`DeadlineChoice.Repeat`) stay in the form, switched off (`DeadlineForm.usesRepeatField`), as the way back until the owner has used the new control.

### A deadline can have its own reminder time
- **Decided (owner):** any deadline can have its own reminder time; the general time in Settings stays the default. *Ora amintirii*, in the deadline's reminder settings and in the deadline form: „Ora obișnuită (09:00)”, showing the general time, or *Altă oră* with a time picker (`DSReminderTimeField`). Choosing the usual one again clears it, so the general time and its later changes reach it again.
- **Decided:** one action, `ObligationActions.setReminderTime(_:minuteOfDay:startedFrom:)`, undoable, laying the edit over the time stored then (`ThreeWay`); in the reminder settings it is a page of its own saved with *Salvează ora*, so a picker's turns make one step. The value is the deadline's existing `reminderMinuteOfDay` (no new database version): it syncs, travels in backups and merges like the deadline's other values, and, as before, it doesn't make the cycle new (`CycleTimes`). One conversion between a picker's moment and a minute of the day (`ReminderClock`), used by Settings too.
- **Left open:** a time moved later on the day a reminder already fired at the old time plans that reminder again at the new time, as a change of the general time already did.

### Recurring deadlines, checked end to end
- **Decided:** every unit and both ways of counting are pinned with tests: completing early, on time or late, in the app and from a reminder, the widget or Siri; month ends kept by a series from its due dates (31 December every two months: 28 February, 30 April, …, 31 August); a snooze moves nothing; reminders for the next cycle at the deadline's own time; Urmează and the widget show the next day.
- **Decided:** counted from its due dates, one "Gata" closes one cycle, so a deadline done after its next due day is still late after it (every cycle is its own, like a month's rent; already the rule for fixed dates). *Alternative:* skip to the first due day after the day it was done, which a chore would want; the person counts a chore from when it was done instead, the new default for days and weeks.
- **Decided:** a widget's "Gata" the app applies later counts from when it was tapped (`OutsideRequest.actedAt`, from the queue's `requestedAt`), never later than now. **Why:** it was applied as done when the app next opened, so a deadline counted from when it was done moved by the delay.
- **Decided:** a series remembers its day of the month only through completions that were one step of its current rule (`RecurrenceEngine.seriesStart(…interval:)`). **Why:** a deadline that went every 10 days from the 5th and was then made monthly from the 25th went to the 5th.
- **Decided:** a reminder lead that would fall on or before the day the cycle began (the due day one step of the rule before, or the fixed date before) is left out of that cycle: `ReminderProfile.forCycle`, read through `Obligation.cycleReminders` by the reminders, the urgency rule (`Obligation.urgencyRule`), Urmează, the widget and the page. It never fires in the past, never counts as a warning missed (no catch-up) and never keeps the deadline urgent for its whole cycle; when no lead fits, it reminds on the day, so a repeating deadline is never silent. The leads stay stored as the person chose them, so a longer repeat uses them again; the reminder settings don't offer a lead that doesn't fit, and one kept says why. *Alternative:* shorten a lead to the longest that fits, which would remind the day after it was done; or drop the leads when the repeat changes, which loses them.
- **Left open:** a counter-based rule and the ITP rule keep every lead (their cycle has no set length here; their leads are short).

### What "Am rezolvat" asks is the deadline's kind's
- **Decided (owner):** from TestFlight: closing a chore ("Udat") asked for a cost and a photo of a document, as if it were a bill. What completing asks is a property of the deadline's kind (`ObligationKind.completion`, a `CompletionShape`: the date, the money and how it is asked, the paper and which paper, a renewed document's new expiry, a note, the section's counter), declared in one place and read by every screen that completes a deadline and by what saving keeps (`CompletionDraft`: a value the kind doesn't ask is never filed). Everything still goes through the same actions (`CaptureFiler.Completion`, `ObligationActions.complete`); there is no second completion path. Nothing stored changes.
- **Decided (owner):** a chore (*routine*, *Doar să nu uit*) is one tap. The deadline page's button says *Gata* and marks it done today, with no sheet; the toast says „Udat: gata. Următorul termen e pe …” with *Anulează* and *Adaugă o notă*, which opens a small note for that completion, kept on its entry in Istoric (`HistoryActions.setNote`, its own step with its own *Anulează*). Closing the note without saving shows the completion's toast again, so its *Anulează* still works. The toast stays 8 seconds instead of 5 when it offers a next step (`DSToast.offerDuration`). On the page a second *Gata* waits while the first one's toast shows, so a double tap never closes the next cycle too.
- **Decided (owner):** *maintenance*: when, *Cât a costat*, the invoice (*Factura*: „De la service sau de la cel care a lucrat.”) and the section's counter where it has one (a car's kilometres, a boat's engine hours: the deadline's own counter, else the section's, `ObligationActions.counter`, which `plannedReading` records in), asked like the visit form's and asking first when it is lower. *payment*: when, *Cât ai plătit*, the bill, filed as a bill as before (`billPlan`, `logBill`, `finishBill`, unchanged). *renewal*: when, the new document (*Actul nou*: „Opțional. Din el iau data la care expiră.”) and *Expiră pe*, filled from the photo and changeable; the day the person leaves wins over the one read (`CaptureFiler.Completion.nextDue`) and goes through the one rule for a new document's date (`renew`).
- **Decided:** the kinds the owner left to decide. *legal* (ITP, a gas check, the rovinietă): when, *Cât a costat* (a fee) and the proof (*Dovada*: „Raportul, certificatul sau chitanța.”), whose date sets the next deadline; no counter, since an ITP report prints its kilometres and capture reads them. *insurance*: when, *Cât a costat* (the premium) and the new policy (*Polița nouă*), whose number, insurer and claims phone fill the section as before. *tax*: when, *Cât ai plătit* and the receipt (*Chitanța*: „Sau dovada plății din aplicația băncii.”). *health*: when, *Cât a costat* (a vet's vaccine, a dentist: optional), a note („Ce s-a făcut, ce urmează.”) and the result (*Rezultatul*: „Analizele, rețeta sau carnetul de vaccinări.”). *Alternative:* health with a note and a result only, as first suggested; rejected because a pet's vaccine and a dentist's check-up are paid, and the field is optional.
- **Decided:** outside the app (a reminder's *Am rezolvat*, the widget's *Gata*, Siri and Shortcuts, `IntentEngine.perform`) every kind still completes in one step, as before; and so do Urmează's swipe and menu, the quick way: a chore's toast there offers the note too. *Alternative:* Urmează's swipe opens the sheet for a kind that asks something; not done, because the swipe is the quick way and the deadline's page has the sheet.
- **Decided:** the deadline's page lists what the deadline actually has: last time's date, cost and note, the policy number and claims phone of its own policy, the owner's class and its documents each only when there is one, so a chore shows none of them unless it has them. A record's note can be added or changed later from its page in Istoric (*Adaugă o notă*, *Schimbă nota*), through the same sheet and action.
