- **Decided:** every question a step asks the person (`StepQuestion`: a counter lower than before, `LowerReadingQuestion`; something of theirs going into an example, `ExampleSectionQuestion`) is asked before its unit of work writes anything. Each question has one rule that changes nothing (`HouseholdMutations.askAboutReading`, `ExampleOwnership.requireReal`). An action that records a reading plans it first (`PlannedReading`, from `AssetActions.plannedReading`, `HistoryActions.plannedReading`, `ObligationActions.plannedReading`), asks, then opens its unit and records exactly what it asked about. A flow that wraps actions in a larger step asks all of their questions before its unit opens: `CaptureFiler.askFirst` asks the review's (the section may be an example, the kilometres an invoice or a report prints, a bill's index), "Am rezolvat"'s and the visit form's (which used to keep the firm before the visit asked). This makes #43's fix for a bill's index the rule. *Alternative:* record the reading first, before anything else, in each action, with a comment on why the order matters; a flow that wraps several actions would still write before the second one asks.
- **Decided:** a filing asks whether its section is an example first, then about a lower counter: the person keeps the example as theirs, then answers the reading, each once.
- **Decided:** `UnitOfWork` stops a test run where a question is thrown after the unit wrote (an assertion, only while tests run), so any test that takes such a path fails where it happens; in the app it is only logged, since the rollback leaves everything readable and the owner installs debug builds on their phone. *Alternative:* stop every debug build, which would crash the owner's phone on a path no test took.
- **Decided:** after any rollback (a failed save too), `UnitOfWork` fetches again every record the unit touched and every record those pointed at, and a unit that changed nothing takes nothing back. A section drawn right after a failed save reads as it was. Tried on GitHub's iOS 27 simulator first: without fetching again, reading a car's deadlines after a failed visit (a unit that failed, and a save that failed) stopped the test run; with it, both read as before. *Alternative:* only ask questions first and leave other failures as SwiftData leaves them, which a full phone could still reach.
- **Decided:** a new architecture rule, `question-outside-its-rule`: `LowerReadingQuestion(…)` and `ExampleSectionQuestion(…)` are made only in their rules' files (and the design system's gallery), so a second, hand-written check can't come back.
- **Why:** a unit taken back after it wrote leaves the records it touched unreadable to SwiftData: reading a section's readings right after the question stopped the test run on the iOS 27 simulator, and could stop the app when the screen draws the section again.

### A document filed again logs one entry
- **Decided:** the same bill filed again (the same document, `review.duplicateAttachmentID`) updates the payment entry it logged in Istoric instead of adding a second one, found through the document's link to it. The entry takes what was read now (a corrected amount, its title and day), moves with the bill to another subscription, and goes when the bill no longer goes to a subscription, as the bill's reading does. Only a payment on a subscription counts as the bill's own entry; any other record the photo was linked to keeps its place, as before. "Anulează" brings it back as it was. *Alternative:* leave the old entry where it was, without its document, which is how the doubling came about.
- **Decided:** a bill's document links to the bill's own entry, not to the completion its reminder's last cycle wrote when the new bill moved it on. That completion closes the previous bill's reminder; the new bill is its own entry. Without this, the second month's bill couldn't find its entry when filed again.
- **Decided:** the same invoice filed again through the visit form on the same section updates the visit it was filed with: its kind, day, cost, rating, firm, lines and kilometres (the old reading goes, the new one is recorded), instead of adding a second visit. A photo kept on another section still moves with none of its old links and makes a visit of its own ("One document, one place"). *Alternative:* ask whether it is the same visit; the invoice is the visit's own paper, so on its own section the answer is always yes.
- **Checked:** a policy filed again doesn't double anything: its deadline already sits on the policy's day, so no second cycle closes.
- **Left:** another photo of the same bill (a second document) still logs a second entry. A history entry keeps the invoice number only in its note, so there is nothing reliable to match on yet.
- **Why:** refiling a bill to correct what was read made Istoric show it twice and its trend count it twice.

### The AI provider reads a meter's index as printed
- **Decided:** the person's AI provider is asked for a meter's index exactly as printed, decimals and all ("12.345,678"), and the answer is read by the one number reader as a meter index from a document (`RomanianNumber`, `CloudSchema.meterIndex`), the same way the phone reads it off the bill. A number that could read two ways ("12.345") follows the Romanian rule and is marked "Verifică"; one the page's text agrees with needs no look (`CloudAnswerMapper`). A JSON number in the answer is taken as the plain machine form it is. *Alternative:* a JSON number with decimals allowed, which leaves the thousands-or-decimals question to the provider and never marks it.
- **Why:** asked as a whole number, a water meter's decimals were lost.

### A bill's meter index is a reading on its subscription
- **Decided:** filing a gas, electricity or water bill records the meter index it prints as a reading, through the one path (`HouseholdMutations.recordReading`, from a document), dated by the bill (its issue date, else the day it is filed) and linked to the bill. `CaptureFilingPlan.meter` decides it and `CaptureFiler` carries it out, in the filing's unit of work, so "Anulează" takes the reading back with the rest. The review says it under what was read: "Indexul 12.345 m³ se trece la „Gaz – ENGIE Romania S.A.”."
- **Decided:** the index is recorded as the review shows it: a value marked "Verifică" is kept only as the person left it there (corrected, the correction; cleared, nothing). Only the review records one, since it is the only place the index is shown. *Alternative:* "Am rezolvat" with a photo of the bill records it too, unseen.
- **Decided:** the reading lives on the subscription the bill matches (`SubscriptionMatcher`), not on the home. The meter belongs to the supply point, and readings are one series per section and unit (`ReadingSeries`): on the home, its gas and its water (both m³) would make one series, and a lower water index would ask about the gas. *Alternative:* on the home, as the known gap was worded, which needs a meter name on every reading to keep the series apart.
- **Decided:** what the meter measures and its unit come from the subscription's service, as data (`ServiceKind.meterKind`, with the kind's own unit): gas and water in m³, electricity in kWh. Never from what the bill prints beside the number. A subscription that doesn't say its service keeps no index, and the review says so ("Indexul nu-l trec: nu știu dacă e de gaz, de curent sau de apă."); internet, TV and phone plans have no meter.
- **Decided:** a bill records one index. Filed again, it keeps the reading it recorded when nothing changed ("Indexul … e deja trecut la …"), replaces it when the index was corrected or the bill moved to another subscription, and takes it away when the bill leaves for no subscription. The same index on the same day is the same reading, so another photo of the same bill records nothing new. No database change: a reading already links to its document (`Attachment.measurement`).
- **Decided:** a lower index than the last one before the bill's day asks, as every reading does ("Citirea e mai mică decât ultima dată. Corectez citirea veche?"); an older bill filed late never asks.
- **Decided:** the subscription's page shows the last index in its hero, under the price, the way a car shows its kilometres: *Ultimul index*, "12.345 m³", "De pe factura din 5 octombrie 2026" (the last reading of the cleaned series in the service's unit, `SubscriptionMeter.last`). Nothing shows before the first reading; no list, no chart. *Alternative:* a row in Date importante beside the codes, where every row copies, or a pocket of its own with every reading.
- **Kept:** deleting the bill from Istoric leaves its reading: the reading belongs to the bill's document, which stays in the binder (`HistoryDeletion`). Deleting the document leaves it too, as the meter's record. *Alternative:* the document takes its reading with it, which the deletion's question would then have to say.
- **Why:** the index on every bill was read and checked ("Bills read by their kind's label table") and then thrown away.

### Screens wait for the event, not for a pause
- **Decided:** the three fixed waits are gone. A sheet asked for over another opens once that one's dismissal has completed (`ShellSheetQueue`: the sheet's `onAppear` says it is on screen, its `onDismiss` that it has gone), not 450 ms later; a sheet replaced before it was ever drawn has nothing to wait for. The notification permission is read again when the pass that asks returns (`SideEffectsCoordinator.askForPermission()`, from the explanation and from Settings) and whenever the app becomes active, not 2 s after the question. Settings opened at a section waits for its list to have rows.
- **Decided:** Settings opened from a link comes up already at the section, with no visible scroll, and keeps it at the top while its rows measure themselves, until the person scrolls (`SettingsLanding`). This is how the system's Settings opens a link. *Alternative:* the animated scroll it had, once the sheet has settled; a sheet's content isn't told when its presentation ends, so that would need a guess again.
- **Decided:** the same sheet asked for while it is up stays up, instead of closing and opening again. Asking runs its pass at once, folding a pass already waiting into it, instead of after the 300 ms that folds saves. `router.hasSheet` (up, closing or about to open) keeps the repair prompt and De verificat's landing from popping up in the gap between two sheets.
- **Left:** two short pauses still wait a set time: the lock window hides 400 ms after its fade starts (`LockWindowController`), and De verificat's landing waits its decided 400 ms beat (`InboxLanding.settle`, see "After sharing"). Debounces (search, notes, the coordinator) and how long a toast or a shown code stays are durations by design, not waits.
- **Why:** a guessed pause is too short on a slow phone or with Reduce Motion, and too long everywhere else.

### One image scaler
- **Decided:** every picture changes size in one place, `ImageScaling`: `scaled(_:toLongEdge:_:)` makes it smaller (`.down`: high-quality interpolation, each side rounded) or larger (`.up`: Lanczos, at most three times), and returns the picture itself when it already fits; `decoded(_:maxLongEdge:)` reads a file at a size, upright whatever the camera wrote. Pages kept, thumbnails, compaction, pages read, the share inbox's pictures, a section's photo, the binder's boxes and the page sent to the AI provider all use it; the copies in `CaptureDocumentStore`, `CloudCaptureExtractor`, `CaptureInput`, `InboxPreviewer`, `PageImageDecoding`, the photo picker and the box thumbnails are gone.
- **Decided:** the page sent to the AI provider rounds its sides like every other picture, where it used to cut the fraction off: its short side can be one pixel longer than before (2400 × 1601 instead of 2400 × 1600), and its long side is always exactly 2400. *Alternative:* keep cutting the fraction for that one path, which is a second rule for the same job.
- **Decided:** a section's photo and the boxes' thumbnails are read from the file at their size instead of decoded whole first, and kept upright in their pixels instead of by a tag. They look the same; a large photo no longer takes its full size in memory to be made small.
- **Why:** three copies drifted (one rounded, one didn't); one scaler with tests keeps sizes, quality and the way up the same everywhere.

### Deleting a section or a document still asks first
- **Decided (owner):** deleting a section or a document keeps asking first, even though both can now be taken back with "Anulează". Deleting a deadline keeps not asking, as "„Anulează” for every step, deletions included" built it. This answers that entry's "Kept" note; nothing in the code changes.
- **Why:** a section or a document can hold far more than one deadline, and the question is the person's last look at what goes.

### A category says how its visits read and how it is offered
- **Decided:** the visit flow's wording (its title and the button's, the checklist's and the provider's headings, the history entry's kind and the trade of whoever did it) and how *Adaugă o secțiune* offers a category (its option's title, *Mașină* for *Mașini*, and its place among the six offered first) are data on the category, declared once in `BuiltInTemplates` (`CategoryBehavior.visit`, `offerTitle`, `shortlistOrder`). Screens read them through `visitWording` and `SectionOffers`; none names a built-in. Built-in catalog version 15 brings them to installed copies.
- **Decided:** they are kept in the category's behaviour, the JSON every category already stores and archives carry, not in new columns, so there is no database change. *Alternative:* fields of their own on `TemplateDefinition` and `CategoryTemplate`, which needs a new schema version for text that only built-ins set.
- **Decided:** the wording is stored as text, like a category's name and summary, written when the catalog installs or upgrades it. *Alternative:* a kind of visit (service, vet, tradesperson, doctor) on the category with the words in code, which keeps code choosing the wording.
- **Decided:** a category of one's own keeps today's plain wording, *Am făcut o lucrare* · *Ce s-a făcut* · *Cine a lucrat*, whatever its switches say, and the form doesn't ask for it. *Alternative:* *Am fost la service* when *Are revizii* is on, or a question in the form; the plain wording fits a boat's service and a piano's tuning alike, and one question fewer.
- **Decided:** what people see is unchanged: the same six, in the same order (*Actele unei persoane* fourth, which the binder's order would put last), then every other category in the binder's order. A category of one's own is never shortlisted unless its data says so. The visit wording no longer follows the hero style: a category of one's own brought by an archive with a car's hero now reads plainly.
- **Decided:** whose a document is, when adding one, is asked among the sections of any category that plays the holder's part, and Urmează's missing bills read the sections of any category that plays a subscription's (`keys(playing:)`), the same set its refresh watches.
- **Decided:** launch saves through `UnitOfWork` (`LaunchData`): the built-ins, the repair list and the household's own deadlines, each its own step, so one that fails takes back only itself and is tried again at the next launch, with nothing shown.
- **Why:** the add menu, the visit flow and one query still asked which built-in a category was, so the catalog's words lived in screens and a category of one's own could never be worded or offered its own way.

### Bills read by their kind's label table
- **Decided:** a bill is read the same way whoever sent it. Its dates, meter index and payment code are the bill kind's own label table (`CaptureKindProfile.billLabels`, read by `BillReader` through `LabelledLayout`); the codes it shares with its contract (client code, POD, CLC, contract number) are `ContractReader`'s table; the total and the invoice number are read as on any invoice (`RomanianFieldExtractor.total`, `CompositeLabels.invoice`). A known provider's name (`UtilityProviders`, a table) only claims the page as a bill and names the provider. Numbers go through `RomanianNumber` as a meter index or money, dates through the one date reader, so a meter index that could read two ways ("Index nou: 12.345") is marked "Verifică" like any other value. This replaces the first bill reader (`RomanianUtilityParser`), kept switched off in `ios/Legacy/` until the new one is proven on real bills.
- **Decided:** a bill's values are pattern readings, not a provider's known format: the AI provider and a decoded barcode outrank them, as on any other document. *Alternative:* keep them deterministic for the seven known providers, as the first reader did, which also made a payment code read off the text count as checked.
- **Decided:** the meter index is marked "Verifică" when it could read two ways, when the bill prints two different new indexes (two registers) or when it is lower than the previous index it prints. The previous index ("Index vechi") is a label of the table, read only for that check, never as the new index. *Alternative:* the first reader's fallback, the previous index as the meter index when no new one is printed.
- **Decided (cautious):** a due date without its label is the latest date in the two months after a labelled issue date, marked "Verifică". *Alternative:* the first reader's, unmarked, and the latest date anywhere on the page when the issue date is missing (dropped).
- **Decided:** a POD is the consumption point's code only, never also the payment code (the first reader gave a Hidroelectrica POD as both); "Energie activă" is no longer read as the meter index, since on a bill it labels the energy billed. The register "1.8.0" still is, for every provider.

### "Anulează" for every step, deletions included
- **Decided:** pausing, resuming, archiving and deleting a deadline, bringing a snoozed one back, deleting a section, a personal document or a paper, and the review's *Salvează* are undoable through the one receipt (`UnitOfWork.performUndoable`, `UndoReceipt`). No second undo path, no database change.
- **Decided:** a delete comes back whole. The receipt reads, just before the save, what the deleted records own by the schema's own cascade rules (`UndoableRecords.ownedRelationships`, read from the models' `@Relationship`, not written a second time) and every record that pointed at them, and works out after the save which of them are gone. So it doesn't depend on when the store applies its delete rules. Reminders, the badge, the widget and the iPhone's search follow the undo's save, as they follow every save.
- **Decided:** a toast whose screen closes (deleting a section, a document or a deadline; the review closing on *Salvează*) is shown by the shell's host, above the tab bar of whichever tab is open (`DSToastCenter`, `\.announce`). Screens that stay keep their own toast. Only the newest toast in a window shows. *Alternative:* one host for every toast, which needs a second one in every sheet anyway.
- **Decided:** a deletion's toast stays 8 seconds (16 with VoiceOver), other toasts 5, since a deletion brings back more and its screen has usually closed. *Alternative:* 5 seconds for all, like before.
- **Decided:** every toast goes when the app goes to the background, and its undo with it: an undo is for the step just taken, and coming back an hour later to a toast that can bring back a deleted section would be a surprise. *Alternative:* keep the toast and its receipt until its time runs out on screen.
- **Decided:** something shared from another app and filed from *De verificat* stays in the share inbox, hidden, while the toast can take the filing back, and comes back with *Anulează*; it leaves for good once the toast is gone (`DSToast.whenGone`, `IntakeInbox.setAside`). A dismissed item works the same way, which replaces the fixed 11-second wait. *Alternative:* remove it at once, as before, and undo leaves the person without the document they shared.
- **Decided:** the deadline's page offers *Pune pe pauză* (under *Schimbă data*) and *Șterge termenul* (at the bottom, like a section's). Deleting a deadline doesn't ask first, since the toast takes it back (docs/COPY_GUIDE.md); archiving keeps no button of its own, since a deletion that can be taken back covers a mistake. *Alternative:* ask first, like a section, or no buttons at all, which leaves pause and delete without any way in.
- **Kept:** deleting a section, a personal document or a paper still asks first, exactly as before. With *Anulează* now there, the copy guide's rule would let those go too; that's the owner's call.
- **Kept:** onboarding's own capture says it was saved without *Anulează*: the toast host is under its full-screen cover.
- **Why:** each of these left the person with no way back but redoing it by hand, and a deleted section took its deadlines, history and documents with it.

### One page per document
- **Decided (owner):** a document is one calm page: its picture first (front and back), "Trimite" and "Rotește" by it, the values below with "Copiază", and "Editează" to correct the values, add the back and read it again. A person's page lists their documents directly.
- **Decided:** the page is the personal document's own: opening the document from a person, from Acte, from search or from its picture (`AppRoute.asset`, or `AppRoute.document` for its current picture) shows the same page (`PersonalDocumentPage`). Its section page and the old document screen stay, switched off (`DocumentPageLayout.isOn`), as the way back. *Alternative:* rebuild only the picture's screen and keep the section page in front of it, which is two pages and a tap more for one document.
- **Decided:** the picture shows one side at a time, at the size of the largest page so nothing moves, with *Fața* / *Spatele* above it for a card's two sides; a longer document's pages are swiped, with page dots. A tap opens the full-screen viewer. *Alternative:* front and back one under the other, both always seen, with a "Rotește" under each.
- **Decided:** "Trimite" (filled, the page's main action) and "Rotește" sit right under the picture, side by side when they fit, not pinned to the bottom edge, so they read as the picture's own and show without scrolling at any text size. *Alternative:* "Trimite" pinned to the bottom with `.dsPinnedAction`.
- **Decided:** "Copiază" is on every value a form asks to be typed (a number, a series, a date, a CNP), never on a choice (the type, the variant): `DocumentPageFacts.copiesValue`. Every copy from a personal document is personal: this phone only, gone after a minute. *Alternative:* "Copiază" on every row.
- **Decided:** the expiry always shows its urgency on the document's page, calm too ("Peste 4 ani"), like the old hero did; lists still show it only when it isn't calm.
- **Decided:** "Editează" is one sheet: the picture's pages on top with *Adaugă spatele* and *Citește din nou*, then the fields with the same editors as any section. Reading fills the form, names what it changed, and offers *Păstrează ce era*; nothing is kept until *Gata*, which saves the values, the back and the text read as one step with "Anulează" (`AssetActions.editDocument`). *Alternative:* reading again opens capture's full review and files it as a new document.
- **Decided:** *Adaugă spatele* is offered only for types printed on both sides, as data (`DocumentType.keepsBackSide`: identity card, licence, the health cards, *Alt document*), never for a bank card (its back shows the security code) and not for a PDF. The back is read with the front as one document; the front's photo and the document's fingerprint stay as they were. *Alternative:* "Adaugă o pagină" on any document.
- **Decided:** *Citește din nou* reads on the phone only, never with the person's AI provider, and keeps the type the person chose (the pages are read as that kind) unless it is *Alt document*. *Alternative:* offer the provider here too, as the review's "Citește mai bine" does.
- **Decided:** a name that is still its type's own ("Alt document – Exemplu Ion") follows a corrected type ("Pașaport – Exemplu Ion"); a name the person chose or changed in the same edit never does (`DocumentNaming.retyped`). *Alternative:* never rename.
- **Decided:** a person's page lists every one of their documents (no "Vezi tot" for them), then at most three papers; each row shows the document's own picture while the documents' category is open, never a bank card's. *Alternative:* the symbol only, as before.
- **Decided:** the read text of a personal document is no longer on its page (the values are its readable form); a paper's page keeps it, folded. *Alternative:* keep it folded at the bottom of every page.
- **Decided:** any other paper (a policy, an invoice, an older picture of a document) opens picture first too, with "Trimite" and "Rotește", then what it is, its dates and where it is filed; it has no "Editează", since what was read off it lives on its section. *Alternative:* an edit for a paper's title and date.
- **Decided:** no change to what is stored: front and back are pages of one document, as before.
- **Why:** getting to an identity card's number took three taps through two pages that each showed half of it; people reach for the card's picture first.

### A document's picture
- **Decided (owner):** a photo picked from the library is cut out automatically: the document is found, cut out and flattened. The photo as taken stays in the binder, and the review offers "Folosește poza întreagă" when the cut-out is wrong.
- **Decided:** what is kept is the photo as taken plus how to show it, not a second picture: the document's four corners (as fractions of the photo, so they stay right when old pages are shrunk), the app's guess of the turn, whether the whole photo reads better, and the person's own choice (`DocumentFraming` on `Attachment.framingData`, database version 6). Every screen, share and thumbnail draws a page through `DocumentPicture`. *Why:* one source of truth for each page; going back to the whole photo loses nothing; nothing more on disk or in backups; and never a second picture of a bank card. *Alternative:* an extra file per page with the cut-out (faster to show, but twice the space and the backup size, and two pictures of the same card).
- **Decided:** the turn comes from reading, where the same cut-out and turn are read: a card that comes out portrait is turned the way more text reads, and a poor reading tries the other three ways up and the whole photo, keeping whichever reads better. The person's turn ("Rotește") is kept apart from the guess and wins over it, now and after any later reading. Pages from the document scanner are kept as the scanner made them.
- **Decided:** a PDF is kept whole and drawn by one renderer (`PDFPageRendering`) at the sharpness asked for: on screen, one drawn pixel for each pixel of the screen at the zoom shown (the viewer draws the part on screen again after zooming); shared as pictures, 300 pixels per inch. The review now shows a PDF's own pages, not its small thumbnail. The renderer follows a page's own rotation, for reading too.
- **Decided:** documents filed before version 6 are left as they are: their pages show whole, as before; they can be turned. *Alternative:* a `DataRepairs` step offering to cut out older photos, which would read every old photo again with Vision.
- **Decided:** "Folosește poza întreagă" is one switch for the whole document in the review, and per page (with "Rotește") in the full-screen viewer, in the review and on a filed document; on a filed document both are undoable steps (`AttachmentActions.turnPage`, `showWholePhoto`), the switch with "Anulează".

### Sending a document
- **Decided (owner):** "Trimite" lets the person choose the front, the back or both, and pictures or one PDF. Nothing is printed on what is sent (no watermark).
- **Decided:** one piece for every screen (`DocumentShareButton`, `documentShare` with a `DocumentShareRequest`): a small choice, then the system share sheet. Only the pages there are are offered: nothing to choose for one page; *Amândouă*, *Doar fața*, *Doar spatele* for two; *Toate paginile* or *Doar prima pagină* for more. It starts with every page, as the document came (a PDF as a PDF, photos as pictures). *Alternatives:* picking any pages of a long document; remembering the last format.
- **Decided:** what goes is the pages as shown (cut out, turned), never what was read or the values. Pictures are JPEGs with no location, camera or date in them. One PDF of photos has a page per picture, as wide as an A4 page; a PDF goes as its own pages (sharp, turned as shown) without the file's title or author.
- **Decided:** files are named after the document's type ("Carte de identitate.pdf", "Carte de identitate – fața.jpg"), else its kind ("Poliță 1.jpg"), else "Act"; never the holder's name or a number.
- **Decided:** a document from a locked category asks for Face ID or the passcode before it goes, every time, even with the session open, without opening or closing it (`BiometricVaultManager.confirmSharing`), as showing a PIN does. *Alternative:* let the open session cover it.
- **Decided:** the files are written for that share only, with full file protection, and removed as soon as the share sheet closes; whatever a closed app left goes at the next share.
- **Decided:** a bank card (`DocumentType.sharedWithCare`) says before it goes that its picture shows the whole number, to send it only to someone the person trusts, and that the bank never asks for it. *Alternative:* no note, or no "Trimite" for a card at all.
- **Decided:** "Trimite" is on filed documents (the document page and its full-screen viewer), not in the review before saving. *Alternative:* offer it in the review too, for "take a photo to send it".

### Policy numbers kept without their number are repaired
- **Decided (owner):** this replaces "policy numbers already kept are left as they are" (below). A repair step, last in the list and asking first (`PolicyNumberPartsStep`), finds a policy number kept with only its series, on any deadline whose facts name a policy number (a car's RCA and CASCO, and a home's policies, since the rule is the same). When the policy filed on the section still has its read text and the rule for values printed in parts reads the whole value there with the same series, it offers that value. Otherwise it only says to read the policy again or type the number, with *Am înțeles* instead of *Corectează*: nothing is guessed. A number with no series is never flagged, since some policies print none.
- **Why:** the owner chose a repair over asking people to re-read each policy; the policy's own text usually still holds the number.

### The bonus-malus class belongs to the person
- **Decided (owner):** the bonus-malus class follows the person's history, not the car's ("a person in an accident would see their class decrease, while a user with no accidents grows their class up to the max"). This replaces the recommendation of a car field in "A digit glued to letters is never an amount" (below).
- **Decided:** it is *Clasă bonus-malus* on the household member, a choice with stable keys over the official scale, worst to best: M8 … M1, B0 (where a new policyholder starts), B1 … B8. Source: Norma ASF nr. 20/2017 privind asigurările auto din România, whose bonus-malus system has a base class B0, eight bonus classes and eight malus classes.
- **Decided:** each RCA keeps its class on the person it insures: the one person whose documents carry the CNP read on the policy, else the member its name matches by the rule that files a holder's documents. It is never kept on a car. With no match, or no name on the policy, it isn't kept and the review says why ("… nu e printre persoanele din dosar. Adaugă persoana și citește polița din nou.").
- **Decided:** the RCA names who it insures in its facts (a new fact role, `policyholder`, the car's *Asigurat RCA*), as the household knows them; its deadline page and the claim screen show "Clasa proprietarului: B6" read from that person, so it is never stored twice. CASCO and home policies name no policyholder.
- **Decided:** the policyholder's CNP is read only on the phone and only to find the person: never shown in the review, kept, filed or asked of the AI provider.
- **Decided:** the catalog moves to version 14; installed copies upgrade at launch, keeping their people and preferences.
- **Decided (owner):** "Clasa proprietarului" is shown on the car's pages even when the person's category is locked with Face ID: the class isn't a secret, though it is read from a locked section.
- **Decided (owner):** a car owned by a company (a CUI on the policy) has no person to keep the class on, so the class isn't kept.
- **Why:** the class moves with the driver, so on a car it would be wrong after a sale or for a second driver.

### The share sheet's confirmation stays until "Gata"
- **Decided (owner):** the confirmation after a share no longer closes by itself after 2.5 seconds. It stays until the person taps "Gata", a `DSPrimaryButton` pinned to the bottom with `.dsPinnedAction`, so nobody misses what happens next ("Atinge notificarea ca să continui…"). While copying, the same place holds "Anulează". The words scroll above the button at any text size.
- **Decided:** the old auto-close is kept switched off (`ShareConfirmation.closesByItself`, with `DS.Motion.confirmationDwell`) as the way back until the owner has seen the new one on a phone. What the confirmation says, phase by phase, is `ShareConfirmation` in `ios/Shared`, so the app's tests pin it.
- **Decided:** so the extension uses the app's own buttons rather than a look-alike, `DS.Space`, `DS.Control`, `DS.Radius`, the full-width buttons and `dsPinnedAction` moved to `ios/Shared`, as motion did before.
- **Decided (owner), kept as built:** the five choices from "After sharing: a notice…" and "Waiting items can be told apart": the notice counts every document waiting; it stays while some of its items still wait; when a route the person opens wins, the landing waits for the next plain return; the preview reads with the quick tiers only; only WhatsApp is named as a sender. The notice's exception to "only the coordinator notifies" is approved.
- **Why:** 2.5 seconds is too short for two sentences for many people, and the second one says what to do next.

### After sharing: a notice, and the app opens at De verificat
- **Decided (owner):** iOS doesn't let a share extension open its app (the workarounds risk rejection), so continuing after a share is a quiet notification plus the app opening at De verificat.
- **Decided:** the extension posts the notice itself, and that is the one exception to "the coordinator is the only path to notifications". A flag left for the coordinator would become a notification only once the app was open, which is when it is no longer needed. The exception is one named file, `ios/Shared/InboxNotice.swift`, the only path the architecture check lets reach the notification center outside the coordinator. It posts only when notifications are already allowed (never asks), never for the practice, without sound; one notice at a time, replaced by the next share. The coordinator withdraws it on every pass once none of the items it announced still waits; the inbox tells it when what waits changes (`ShareInbox.didChangeNotification`). It isn't withdrawn when only some of its items are filed: replacing a shown notification would show it again.
- **Decided:** the confirmation says what happens next: "Am primit 2 acte" and "Atinge notificarea ca să continui. Te așteaptă în aplicație, la De verificat." (or "Deschide Dosar cu Șină ca să continui…" without a notice). It counts documents, not files. The notice counts every document waiting, not only this share's: they all wait for the same step. *Alternative:* only this share's, which would need the app's "seen" to live in the App Group.
- **Decided:** a tap opens Adaugă › De verificat (`AppRoute.intake`) through the navigator, so after unlocking. While the app is open, the notice goes to Notification Center without a banner.
- **Decided:** opening the app from the background with something De verificat hasn't shown lands there once (`InboxLanding`), after 400 ms so a tapped reminder or link reaches the navigator first. A route the person is opening wins, and the landing waits for the next plain return; nothing moves while something is open over the tabs (a sheet, a form, an alert, onboarding) or for the practice. "Seen" is De verificat on screen, remembered on the phone as the newest arrival shown. *Alternative:* land on every return while something waits; rejected as nagging.
- **Why:** someone who shares a bill from Mail and is left in Mail, with nothing that says where it went, often never finds it again.

### Waiting items can be told apart
- **Decided:** each waiting item is read once on the phone when it arrives (`InboxPreviewer`), with the same engine as every capture, but only its first page and the quick tiers: no on-device model (battery, for a title) and never the person's AI provider. Nothing goes into the document store. What it learned (kind, title, category, pages, whether a picture was kept) is a versioned file beside the manifest (`InboxPreview`), written by the app only; the manifest itself didn't change. A preview it can't use is made again, never set aside.
- **Decided:** a row's title is what it is once read, else the file's cleaned name, else the app its name says it came from (only WhatsApp names files unmistakably), else what it is made of. Under it: the file's name when the title is the reading, "N pagini" from two pages, and when it arrived. One place decides the words (`InboxWording`).
- **Decided:** names follow the outside rules (`SearchPrivacy.outsideText`: no CNP, card number or sealed value). While a section lock is closed, an item whose reading belongs to a locked category is *Act protejat*, with no name, picture or file name, and opening it asks for Face ID first; an item not read yet names nothing while a lock is closed.
- **Decided:** a first-page picture is kept only when the reading is sure the page can't be a bank card (`PhoneOnlyReading`, the rule the AI provider follows); a page that couldn't be read gets none. A bank card's photo shows its whole number.
- **Why:** two PDFs shared one after the other both read "PDF importat" with a time, and the person couldn't tell which was which.

### A series and a number are one value
- **Decided:** a value printed in parts (a policy's series and number, an identity card's, an invoice's, a contract's, a talon's CIV) is read by one rule: its shape is data (`CompositeValue`), its labels a table, read by `LabelledLayout.composite`. It reads one line, inline "Seria … nr. …", separate lines, side by side, columns, and one header over both parts on lines of their own (an RCA's green card). The value is written one way: the parts joined, "Nr." dropped ("RO/00/X00/XX 123456789", "XT123456").
- **Decided:** a number is never another field's: it follows its own label or its series, and the labels of other fields that start the same way ("Nr. înmatriculare", "Nr. contract", "Nr. identificare", "Serie șasiu", "Serie CIV", "Serie contor") are labels of their own.
- **Decided:** a document keeps one number of its own (`CaptureKindProfile.numberKey`). A "document number" a reader gives a policy, an invoice or a contract is folded into that one and never shown as a second "Serie și număr"; a series in one and a number in the other become one value. This is the owner's RCA report.
- **Decided:** readers agree on a value written differently, or read in part; a part never beats the whole, whoever read it, and two parts read by different readers are joined (`ReadingArbiter.agreedValue`). Two different whole values on one page are marked "Verifică".
- **Decided (cautious):** a contract's number stays one code, without joining a series to it, so it still matches the codes subscriptions already keep. *Alternative:* join a printed series too, with a repair step for the codes kept.
- **Decided (cautious):** a number alone under a policy's own label ("Nr. poliță: 123456789") is kept as the value, since some policies print no series; it isn't marked. *Alternative:* mark it "Verifică" on an RCA, whose series is always printed.
- **Decided:** a talon's CIV series and number fill the car's "Serie CIV".
- **Decided:** policy numbers already kept are left as they are: no repair step guesses a missing number. *Alternative:* a repair that lists RCA numbers kept without their number, for the person to fix.
- **Why:** an RCA prints its series and number apart; one reader took the series and another the number, into two fields, and each patch for one layout missed the next.

### A digit glued to letters is never an amount
- **Decided:** in the one amount rule, a digit touching letters (B6, R1, M2, 5W30) is never an amount, unless the letters are its currency ("350lei"). A label's amount is the first one after it followed by its currency, else the last; an amount on the next line counts only when it stands there alone.
- **Why:** an RCA's "Prima de asigurare 1234.56 LEI, Clasă Bonus-Malus: B6" gave a price of 6.
- **Open for the owner:** the bonus-malus class is read and shown in the review but kept nowhere. *Recommendation:* a "Clasă bonus-malus" field on the car, filled from each RCA, with a catalog upgrade; not added without the owner's yes, since it changes the car's category.

---

## 2026-11-03

### The last open questions, answered
- **Decided (owner):** the card-number rule of the privacy check stays strict: any number that passes the card check digit is refused unless it is a published test number, so a made-up IMEI in a test must fail that check digit (or be built when the test runs).
- **Decided (owner):** the bill choices stay as built: a bill picks a home by itself only when street, number and flat match and no other home is close (otherwise "Verifică"); the only home still takes a bill supplied elsewhere, marked "Verifică"; turning direct debit on pauses the bill reminder rather than deleting it; the supply address isn't stored on subscriptions outside a home for now.
- **Why:** the strict rule errs toward keeping real numbers out of a public repository; the bill choices are cautious until they are tried on real bills, and each is one rule that is easy to change.

### A category's service says how often
- **Decided (owner):** this replaces "the service numbers are the car's rule by another counter" in the entry below. When *Are revizii* is on, the form asks how often, by the counter chosen: *La câți km?*, *La câte ore?* or *La câte luni?*, starting at the car's numbers (10.000 km, 100 hours, 12 months). The same field is in Setări › *Categoriile tale*.
- **Decided:** the interval lives only in the category's service blueprint, as its rule (`.metric(every:)`, or `.interval` in months, whole years written as years), and is read back from it (`CategorySwitches.Service`). Changing only the interval changes that blueprint's rule in place; the counter field and its values stay.
- **Decided:** changing it later asks before it touches running deadlines (*Folosește noul interval* or *Lasă-le cum sunt*), in the same undoable step as the other switches. A deadline that takes the new interval keeps the day it is due; the new interval counts from the next service (`ObligationActions.setRecurrence`). A paused deadline turned back on takes the interval the category has then.
- **Decided:** the time option reads *După timp* ("Fără contor, de obicei o dată pe an"), since it can be every six months. *Alternative:* keep *O dată pe an* as its name.
- **Decided (owner):** kept as built, from the entry below: no switch for a person's documents; *Are facturi și contracte* plays a home's part for capture; turning *Are revizii* on adds no deadline by itself (each section's *Alt termen* offers it); changing a category lives in Setări › *Categoriile tale*.
- **Why:** a boat's engine is serviced at 50 or 200 hours as often as at 100; a fixed number would have been wrong for most of them.

### A category of one's own answers a few plain questions
- **Decided (owner):** making a category of one's own (and changing it later) asks a few plain questions instead of a list of pockets: *Are acte* (on by default), *Are facturi și contracte*, *Are revizii* (*O dată pe an*, *După kilometri*, *După orele de motor*) and *Câte o cutie pentru fiecare* or *O cutie pentru toate*. The first version's pocket list is kept switched off (`CustomSectionStep.offersPocketList`) until the questions are proven.
- **Decided:** each answer is the data a built-in has, written to and read back from the category (`CategorySwitches`): the Acte and Abonamente pockets, the box (`CategoryBehavior.box`), the home part for bills (`CategoryBehavior.Role.home`, so capture files a bill into a subscription under it), a counter field as the hero value (one key per unit) and a service blueprint like the car's. Nothing stores the switches, so there is one source of truth, no second mechanism and no database change; backups already carry all of it.
- **Decided:** a switch touches only what it owns, and turning one off only hides. A change that touches running service deadlines (or paused ones it turns back on) asks first, and the whole change is one undoable step through `UnitOfWork.performUndoable`. Built-in categories have no switches.
- **Decided (owner):** "Are facturi și contracte" makes the category play a home's part for capture. With a home and such a category, a first bill whose codes no place keeps yet goes where its supply address says, and otherwise is offered to the person instead of guessed, since either place can have it. *Alternative:* a part of its own for "place with contracts but no address"; not worth a second role while bills can say their address.
- **Decided (owner):** no switch for "its items are a person's documents". Acte already files any document under a person (*Alt act* for one without a type), so a second category doing it would be a second path. *Alternative, if the owner wants it:* a fifth switch setting `role: .personalDocument` and `listing: .byHolder`; adding a document from such a category's page would then need to follow the category instead of Acte.
- **Decided, then replaced:** the service numbers were the car's rule by another counter: 10.000 km, or 100 engine hours, or a year, whichever comes first. Since "A category's service says how often" (above), they are asked, starting at these.
- **Decided (owner):** turning *Are revizii* on adds no deadline to sections already there; each section's *Alt termen* offers it.
- **Decided (owner):** changing them later is in Setări › *Categoriile tale*, beside *Pe ecranul Dosar*.
- **Why:** the behaviour was data since "What a category does is data", but only built-ins could set it; a person's own boat had no engine hours, no service and no place for its berth contract.

### A bill's supply address and its direct debit
- **Decided:** bills and contracts read the supply address ("Adresa locului de consum", "Adresa loc de consum", "Loc consum", "Adresa de livrare", "Adresa punctului de consum", "Adresa de instalare"…) with one label table (`ContractReader+Supply`), never provider code. The payer's addresses ("Adresa de facturare", "de corespondență", "Domiciliu", "Sediu") and the consumption point's codes ("Cod loc de consum") are labels of their own, so they are never read as the supply address; with only a billing address printed, nothing is read.
- **Decided:** addresses are compared part by part (`PostalAddress`): the street's name (an abbreviation like "Gh." counts), its number, block, staircase, apartment, sector and town; diacritics, "Str."/"Strada", "B-dul"/"Bulevardul", "nr." and spacing don't matter. A part both print that differs is another place. The same place means the same street and number and, at the most precise level either one names (the apartment, else the staircase, else the block), the same unit.
- **Decided (cautious, one rule: `SubscriptionMatcher.house(at:among:)`):** when no code decides, a home at the same place is picked by itself only when it is the only one and no other home is even likely. With another home likely too, or only one likely home, that home is suggested and the review marks it "Verifică" ("Adresa locului de consum seamănă cu cea a locuinței, dar nu e chiar aceeași."). Two homes at the same place, two likely ones, or none: the person picks, as before. Codes still come first. With only one home, it is kept as before, marked "Verifică" when the bill is supplied at an address that clearly isn't it.
- **Decided:** a home without an address takes the one its bill is supplied at; an address the person wrote is never changed.
- **Decided:** "debit direct" is read only from statements that the bill *is* paid so ("Factura se achită prin debit direct", "Modalitate de plată: Debit direct", "Debitare directă: activă", "Domiciliere: DA", "Suma va fi debitată din contul…"), and "Plata prin debit direct" only when it stands alone on its line. A "no" ("Nu aveți debit direct"), an offer ("Activează debitul direct", "Poți plăti prin debit direct"), a question, or a list of ways to pay never counts. The AI provider's answer goes through the same rule (`ContractReader.statesDirectDebit`).
- **Decided:** what the bill says is only offered: "Plătesc prin debit direct" in the review's "Când te anunț", off until the person turns it on. On, no reminder to pay is made and the subscription keeps the setting.
- **Decided:** turning direct debit on, in Editează or from a bill, pauses the subscription's "Factura de plătit" (`BillReminders`, run by `AssetActions.update`, through `ObligationActions.pause`); the toast says "Nu-ți mai amintesc să plătești factura: o plătește banca." with "Anulează", which takes back the edit and the reminder together. Turning it off brings back a reminder whose day hasn't passed; one whose day passed stays paused, and the next bill takes it up again on its own day instead of making a second. Pausing, not deleting, keeps the reminder's history and is the way back. The missing-bill note doesn't depend on it.
- **Decided:** `ObligationActions.pause` and `resume` return their undo receipt.
- **Why:** with two homes, a first bill whose codes no home kept waited for the person, though the address on every bill says where it goes; a reminder to pay a bill the bank pays is noise, and a bill often says so. Bills also print the payer's address and advertise direct debit, so both are told apart by rule, and nothing is switched on without the person.

### A reviewed past commit doesn't block the card-number check
- **Decided (owner):** a past commit that the card-number rule flags, but that holds nobody's real number and was fixed in a later commit, is recorded in `scripts/privacy-reviewed-commits.txt` (commit id and reason, never a value) instead of rewriting the shared history.
- **Why:** rewriting the integration branch would force every open branch built on it to be rebuilt, and a pushed commit stays in GitHub's caches anyway; a reviewed, recorded exception keeps the rule strict for everything new.

### Bank cards stay on the phone; a cazier is typed in by its issue date
- **Decided (owner):** a bank card is never sent to the AI provider: not on "Citește mai bine", not by "Citește automat", not by a reading again after pages are added. This replaces "never sent to the AI provider without asking" for bank cards in "Cazier, the European health card and bank cards, from a photo" (below). Which documents stay on the phone is data on their type (`DocumentType.readOnPhoneOnly`, only the bank card), and one rule, `PhoneOnlyReading`, decides whether a capture may be one.
- **Decided (owner):** when the phone can't be sure a page isn't a bank card, it is treated as one: the kind read, or a kind another reader saw, is a bank card; the page shows a card number, whole or already masked; it carries the words only a card prints ("VALID THRU", "GOOD THRU": the heaviest signals of the card's own templates, so there is no second list); or the person is adding one (the capture started from a bank card's page, including "Am rezolvat" on its deadline).
- **Decided:** the rule is applied where reading by the provider is decided, and nowhere else: the engine checks what the phone read of the very pages before anything is sent (so a reading again with added pages is covered, and it says `readOnPhoneOnly` instead of a failure); "Citește automat" neither sends nor asks; the capture session doesn't ask the reader for the provider; the review shows no "Citește mai bine" and says only "Cardurile bancare se citesc doar pe telefon." The schema still asks the provider for only four digits, for a page the phone couldn't recognise at all.
- **Decided (owner):** "Scriu eu" for a cazier asks "Eliberat pe" (what is printed on it), for every variant. A cazier judiciar's "valabil până pe …" is worked out six months later by the documents category's own derivation, the one a photo and Editează use, and the line under the date says "E valabil până pe …, la 6 luni de la eliberare." (one wording, `ReminderWording.validityOpening`). A cazier fiscal or auto keeps its issue date and says there is no reminder. Which types are dated by their issue is derived from the validity data (`DocumentType.isDatedByIssue`: the type or a variant has a validity from its issue), not a list of its own.
- **Decided (owner), settled with nothing to build:** a cazier fiscal or auto gets no reminder until a rule is confirmed; a cazier judiciar reminds a week before and on the day, as "Doar să nu uit"; the national health card stays typed in only, with no template; bank card and cazier deadlines made before keep the reminders they were made with ([30, 7], legal), with no repair step. These answer the questions in HANDOFF from the cazier, health card and bank card work, and are pinned by `SettledDecisionsTests`.
- **Decided (owner):** the privacy check rejects card numbers: any added run of 13 to 19 digits that passes the card check digit (Luhn), whole or in groups of three digits or more, as cards print them. Only the published test numbers listed in `scripts/privacy-check.sh` pass. Digits inside a longer word or code (a hash, an IBAN, an identifier) aren't counted, and a made-up CNP (county code 99, as AGENTS.md asks) isn't taken for a card.
- **Open for the owner:** an IMEI carries the same check digit, so a new line with a made-up IMEI that passes it is stopped too. *Alternative:* count only runs that start like a card network's numbers, as the app's own masking does.
- **Why:** a card's photo shows its whole number, and the phone reads everything a card carries; a rule that lives on the type and is checked where sending is decided can't be skipped by a new path to the provider.

### Cazier, the European health card and bank cards, from a photo
- **Decided:** three more personal documents are read from a photo the way identity documents are: each is a template (`IdentityTemplates`) and a kind profile (`CaptureKindProfile`), not new reading code. A cazier (judiciar, fiscal or auto, told apart by its own words and kept as the type's variant), the European health insurance card, and a bank card (debit or credit when the card says so). They are personal documents (`CaptureDocumentKind.isPersonalDocument`): filed under the person they belong to, named "Cazier judiciar – Manole Corina", their read text never searched, never sent to the AI provider without asking.
- **Decided:** a cazier keeps the holder's name, CNP, number, issue date, issuer and what it says ("Nu figurează în cazierul judiciar"), as printed. The layout couldn't be checked against the specimen in Hotărârea Guvernului nr. 345/2010, so its labels are the usual wordings, and the AI provider is asked for the same fields.
- **Decided:** a cazier judiciar is valid six months from its issue. It is data (`DocumentType.validityMonthsAfterIssue`), from Romania's own entry in the European Commission's repository of public documents ("Certificatele de cazier judiciar sunt valabile 6 luni de la data eliberării", entry 431105, under Legea nr. 290/2004). The documents category works the expiry out from the issue date (`FieldDerivation.validityAfterIssue`), so a photo, "Scriu eu" and an edit agree; the review says "E valabil până pe …, la 6 luni de la eliberare." A cazier fiscal or auto keeps its issue date and has no reminder: no rule was confirmed for them.
- **Decided:** a cazier reminds a week before and on the day, as "Doar să nu uit" (comfort), not as a legal deadline: it is asked for a purpose, not renewed by law, so an expired one is only history and one about to end never crowds *Acum*.
- **Decided:** whether a deadline that passed is still worth making follows the profile the section's values give (`isWorthScheduling(on:today:values:)`), not the blueprint's default. Before, every document type counted as "renewed by law", so an expired bank card or cazier read from a photo would have been overdue.
- **Decided:** the European health card is read field by field as the EU sets it (Decision No S2 of the Administrative Commission, OJ C 106, 24.4.2010) and CNAS prints it (Ordinul CNAS nr. 559/2006, anexa 1): 3 Nume, 4 Prenume, 5 Data nașterii, 6 the CNP, 7 the institution, 8 the card's number, 9 Data expirării. It is filed as `documentType.euHealthCard`, so *Pleci din țară* reads the same expiry field.
- **Decided:** the national health card is a document type the person adds with its holder and expiry; it has no template, because its printed fields couldn't be confirmed against an official specimen.
- **Decided (owner):** a bank card keeps its bank, its network (Visa, Mastercard…), debit or credit, the last four digits and the expiry, shown as "Visa •••• 4821, expiră 08/28", and reminds a month before it expires and on the day. Never its full number, a security code or a PIN.
- **Decided:** the full number is kept out by one set of rules, `PaymentCardNumbers`, applied wherever text enters or leaves: every capture result (strict on a bank card: any long run of digits), what a document's text keeps, every field a section saves, every import, search and the iPhone's search. Anywhere else a number counts as a card's only by its shape (14 to 19 digits, a card network's first digits, the Luhn check or groups of four), so a CNP, an IMEI or a health card's number stays. The last four digits aren't searched or shown outside the app either.
- **Decided (owner):** a bank card's photo is kept only when the person turns on "Păstrează și poza cardului" (off by default), in the review and in "Am rezolvat"; it shows the whole number.
- **Decided:** a captured kind's document kind comes only from its profile; the second switch that repeated it is gone.
- **Why:** these papers come in as photos like the rest, and people need what's on them for forms; a card number is the one value the binder must never hold, so it is removed by rule at every door rather than trusted to each screen.
- **Open for the owner:** see HANDOFF, "Questions for the owner".

### The iPhone's search and the app lock; people's names
- **Decided (owner):** while the whole app is locked, nothing of it appears in the iPhone's search: the app's index is deleted and written again when the lock is turned off. Household members' names and their documents' titles appear whenever their category isn't locked, like everything else.
- **Why:** someone who locks the whole app expects nothing of it to show outside, as Siri already behaves; a category lock is the finer control for people and their papers.

### The iPhone's search
- **Decided:** section names, deadlines ("RCA – VW Golf", with its due day under it) and document titles appear in the iPhone's search (Spotlight). Nothing else: no field values, notes, history, firms, or text read off a document.
- **Decided:** a category behind Face ID shows nothing there at all, titles included, whether or not it is open now. Turning a lock on removes what was shown. This is stricter than Caută, where a locked section still shows its title.
- **Why:** Caută is inside the app, which the person has opened; Spotlight is reachable by anyone holding the unlocked phone, and showing a title is a promise the lock doesn't make.
- **Decided:** examples never appear (deadlines come from the one list of what may leave the app, `ObligationSnapshot.fetchForOutside`); neither does a closed one-off deadline or an archived document. A title never carries a CNP-shaped number, a sealed value, or a value its category keeps out of search (a document number, with or without spaces); a title that is only such a number isn't shown. The rules are `SearchPrivacy.mayShowOutside` and `outsideText`, beside the in-app search's, so there is one definition.
- **Decided:** "Arată în căutarea iPhone-ului" in Settings, on by default. Off deletes the app's whole index.
- **Replaced (2026-11-17, see "The iPhone's search and the app lock" and "A locked section behaves the same on every surface"):** ~~the app lock doesn't change what appears, the same as widgets and reminders; opening a result waits for Face ID.~~ While the app lock is on, nothing of the app is in the iPhone's search. Widgets and reminders are what the app lock doesn't change: they keep naming the deadline and its section, and only a locked category's use the generic words.
- **Decided:** Core Spotlight items, not App Intents' entity indexing. Entity indexing would need new section and document entities with queries and open intents, which Shortcuts could list outside the app lock, and the words that leave the app would come from each entity's display. Items keep one builder (`SpotlightCatalog`) in charge of every word, the opt-out is one call, and a result opens through the same link parser as everything else. A deadline's item still points to its Siri entity on iOS 27. The index is readable only while the phone is unlocked.
- **Decided:** one indexer, `SpotlightIndexer`, on the coordinator's hook. It writes only what changed, from a ledger of fingerprints on the phone (no titles), and rewrites everything when the ledger and the index disagree.

### "Altă zi…" is a page, not a second sheet
- **Decided:** in "Când te anunț", "Altă zi…" pushes a page inside the sheet, with a back button, instead of opening a sheet on top. Back adds nothing; "Adaugă" adds the day and comes back.
- **Why:** one sheet with a way back reads like the system's own settings sheets; a sheet on a sheet is easy to lose track of.

### Situations live where they belong
- **Decided (owner):** a car's page has a *Situații* group with *Vinzi mașina* and *Ai avut o daună*; a home's page has *Ai avut o daună*; *Pleci din țară* is on a person's page and on Urmează. Each opens a checklist: what's ready, what's missing and what to bring.
- **Decided:** the situations are listed once, `ReadinessSituation`, which says where each is offered (by what the category does, not by its key: a trip on a household member, a sale on a section that keeps an RCA, a claim on any section whose deadlines keep a policy's facts) and where it opens. A "Pregătește-te pentru…" section in Dosar would be a loop over it.
- **Decided:** every rule is in `ReadinessEngine`; the screens draw its outcomes. A row says why in plain words and its tap opens the document or deadline, or adds what's missing through the flow that adds it: a person's document photo first, a paper on the section photo first (one modifier, `photoFirstCapture`, now also behind *Adaugă poza actului*), the deadline form with that deadline already chosen, or the section's edit sheet.
- **Decided:** inside the EU an identity card is enough until its printed expiry, and a passport stands in for it until its printed expiry too; outside the EU a passport must be good for travel, by its cut-off, until the trip ends. The European health card is checked only inside the EU. The green card counts as included with a valid RCA (Romanian RCA policies carry it), or as a card filed on its own. The vignette is needed to reach the border and to come back: amber when it ends during the trip, red when it has ended before.
- **Decided:** a claim with two policies (RCA and CASCO, PAD and the optional one) asks *Ce asigurare folosești*, each with when it applies, and starts on the first the binder follows. A claim and a sale read only their own section, so a car's claim doesn't need Acte opened at the roadside; the driver's licence and identity card are listed to bring, not checked.
- **Decided:** a checklist that reads a category behind Face ID names nothing until it is opened (`ReadinessEngine.readsSensitive`, the vault's one session). A trip reads people, their documents and health cards, cars and pets, so with Acte locked (the default) it asks first.
- **Decided:** the trip is kept on this phone in its preferences (`TripPlanStore`) until the day after it ends, so the checklist opens where the person left it. It isn't a record: no database change, not in backups, sync or search. *Alternative:* a stored trip (a new database version) if trips should travel with backups or sync to another device.
- **Decided:** the trip's days are two date fields, *Pleci pe* and *Te întorci pe*, empty until the person picks them; the checklist shows once they say *Vezi ce-ți trebuie*, with the trip at the top and *Schimbă călătoria*. *Alternatives:* one range picker on a calendar (fewer taps, harder to read at large text), or dates filled in with next week (checklist at once, but a list for days nobody chose).
- **Decided:** things the binder can't check (the contract, what to photograph) are rows the person ticks off on the screen; the ticks aren't kept.
- **Open for the owner:** a home's claim tells PAD from the optional policy by what happened (*Cutremur, inundație sau alunecare de teren* vs the rest); the person still chooses. Asking "Ce s-a întâmplat?" first and choosing for them is the alternative.
- **Why:** the readiness engine existed with nothing on screen, and the moments it is for (a trip, a sale, an accident) are when people look for the papers.

### The travel cut-off follows the document, and the usual reminders can come back
- **Decided (owner):** the travel cut-off always follows the document type, even on a deadline whose reminders the person set. The cut-off is a fact about the document, not a preference. The person's leads, consequence, grace and follow-up stay; only the travel margin (and so the day the leads count from) changes. It is the one rule, `DeadlineReminders`, so Editează, capture's filing and the repair list's travel cut-off step all do it; the repair step now also offers a marked deadline whose margin isn't its document's, and gives it only the margin. This answers the first "Open for the owner" in "A deadline's reminders follow its document" and replaces "a marked deadline keeps its reminders whatever the section becomes" there, for the margin only.
- **Decided (owner):** no separate reset button. Reminders the person set stay theirs until they edit them again, and editing them again is where the way back is. The deadline page shows only what it reminds, in words, and "Schimbă când te anunț", which opens the reminder settings in their own sheet (`ReminderSettingsSheet`: the leads, "Altă zi…", the follow-up). The sheet offers "Revino la cele obișnuite" when the person set the reminders and they differ from what the document gives, with the usual ones in a sentence under it. The tap puts them back, clears `remindersCustomized` and says what they are in an undo toast. One action, `ObligationActions.restoreUsualReminders`, undoable through the step's receipt. Each change in the sheet saves at once, as the page's settings did, so there is nothing to discard. This answers the second "Open for the owner".
- **Decided (owner):** the settings that were on the page stay in the code, switched off (`ObligationPage.editsRemindersInline`), as the way back until the owner has used the sheet.
- **Decided:** the mark means the reminders differ from the usual ones. Ticking exactly the usual ones back by hand clears it (`DeadlineReminders.isCustomized`), so the deadline follows its document again and the option isn't offered for reminders that are already the usual ones. A deadline not made from a category's blueprint has no usual ones: it stays marked when changed, and offers nothing.
- **Why:** a passport whose leads the person changed and that turned out to be an identity card kept counting from a cut-off it doesn't have; and once marked, a deadline had no way back to following its document.

### A deadline's reminders follow its document
- **Decided:** a deadline made from a blueprint reminds as the blueprint says for its section's values. When a value that profile depends on changes (a document's type, a pet's species: whatever the blueprint's `reminderVariants` reads), the deadline takes the new profile whole: leads, consequence, grace, follow-up and travel margin. One rule, `DeadlineReminders`, run by `AssetActions.update`, so Editează and capture's filing do the same thing. Changing an identity card to a passport gives it the passport's reminders and cut-off at once, and changing it back takes them away. A change the profile doesn't read (the expiry, the name) leaves the reminders alone.
- **Decided:** reminders the person set themselves stay. Changing a deadline's leads or its follow-up marks it (`Obligation.remindersCustomized`); a marked deadline keeps its reminders whatever the section becomes. It is stored, so the database is at version 5 (version 4 frozen, a lightweight stage and a test that opens a version 4 store), and it travels in backups.
- **Decided:** deadlines saved before version 5 are not marked: nobody knows which ones the person changed. Their reminders follow the next change of type or species, which is what that change asks for.
- **Decided:** the repair list's travel cut-off step is a check of the same rule. It offers a deadline that isn't marked and whose travel margin isn't its document's, either way (a passport saved before margins, or a document that stopped being a passport before reminders followed the type), and applying gives it what Editează would. This replaces "The person's grace and follow-up stay as they set them" in "A passport is good for travel until six months before it expires": the repair now gives the type's grace and follow-up too, because one path does both. The entry asks first and says when it will remind; it doesn't name the grace or the follow-up, which only change for a passport whose follow-up the person had turned off.
- **Open for the owner:** a marked deadline keeps its travel margin when its type changes, so a passport whose leads the person changed and that turns out to be an identity card still counts from a cut-off. The margin is never chosen by hand, so it could follow the type even for a marked deadline. Recommended: yes, keep the person's leads, grace and follow-up and let only the margin follow.
- **Open for the owner:** once marked, a deadline can't go back to following its document. A "Revino la cele obișnuite" on its reminder settings would clear the mark.
- **Why:** changing a document's type in Editează left its deadline with the old type's reminders and no travel cut-off; only the repair list noticed afterwards.

### Units are known by stable keys
- **Decided:** a reading, a number field, a counted deadline and capture say their unit with one type, `MeasurementUnit`: a stable key (`km`, `h`, `kwh`, `m3`, `gcal`, `kg`, `mgdl`, `mmhg`, `dpt`, `cm3`, `hp`, `m2`, `months`, `shots`) and a label shown to people ("km", "kWh", "m³", "CP", "luni", "cafele"). Code compares keys, never labels: readings are grouped by key, a counted deadline finds its counter by key, a lower kilometre reading asks by key.
- **Decided:** reading accepts every form the app has written: the key, the label (also in the app's language), capture's "mc" and the number reader's "m3", in any case, with or without superscripts. Older data and backups open as they are. A unit the person typed another way ("ore motor") is theirs and stays as written; it is not guessed into the catalog.
- **Decided:** a repair step that runs by itself rewrites stored readings, counted deadlines and the person's own categories to keys. It touches only the unit, in place, so nothing a newer version wrote is lost. Built-in categories get keys from the catalog (version 12). Backups write keys and read both forms (`dosar-v3.md`, Units).
- **Decided:** the type lives in the app (`Models/Values/MeasurementUnit.swift`), not in `ios/Shared` as the plan said: nothing outside the app shows a unit, and moving it there is one file when a widget needs one.
- **Why:** units were display text used as identity: capture's "mc" and a template's "m³" made two series of the same meter, and a translated label ("cafele") would have split a counter from its deadlines.

### One form for a new deadline, one flow for a new section
- **Decided:** a section's *Alt termen* and *Adaugă › Scriu eu* open the same `DeadlineForm`. A deadline of the person's own asks what it is, how important it is and whether it repeats; its kind comes from the importance (legal, payment, health, routine) and its reminders from that kind's default profile (`DeadlineChoice`). The fixed "a week before and on the day, financial" profile the section's sheet gave every deadline is gone. *Scriu eu*'s *Secțiune nouă* opens the binder's *Adaugă o secțiune* flow (category, essentials, deadlines) instead of a name-only form.
- **Open for the owner:** the importance starts on *Doar să nu uit*, as *Scriu eu* already did. Starting with nothing chosen would make the person pick every time.
- **Why:** the same "Plata asigurării" reminded differently depending on where it was typed, and read "Expiră în…" because every deadline from a section was a renewal.

### Rows give way by measuring, not by text size
- **Decided:** one layout, `DSAdaptiveStack`, puts a row's pieces side by side when they fit at their natural width and stacks them otherwise, at any text size and screen width. It replaces the `ViewThatFits` copies and the "accessibility size, so stack" checks in rows, value rows and button rows. Value buttons stack under each other when even they don't fit side by side. Status badges and chips wrap onto a second line instead of being cut off. The binder's grid still drops to one column at the accessibility sizes; that is a grid, not a row.
- **Decided:** tap sizes are tokens (`DS.Control`): 44 pt for anything tapped, and a test measures the DS buttons at every text size. A small action beside a row is `DSCompactButton`.
- **Why:** a size check stacked short rows that fitted and left long ones to clip at the sizes just below it.

### One way to fold text and find a keyword
- **Decided:** `RomanianText.fold` is the one fold (case, diacritics with both ş and ș, wide forms, no-break spaces, the typographic minus) for search, choices, component matching and capture. `CaptureText.containsWord` is the one keyword match for every capture reader and the classifier: folded, phrase-aware, never the middle of a word. Words of four letters or fewer match whole; longer ones may take a Romanian ending. A word whose longer forms mean something else is listed to match only whole (today "index", not "indexare").
- **Why:** three folds gave three answers, and substring matching read "neachitat" as "achitat", skipped "Control frâne" as a "cont" line and counted "indexare" as a meter index.

### Search keeps its own index, away from the main thread
- **Decided:** `SearchIndexer` holds the index, one per database, on a context of its own. It reads everything once; after each save it reads again only the records that save changed and those that show them. A category change, or a save that can't say what it changed, reads everything.
- **Why:** the screen rebuilt the whole index on the main thread after every save, so typing stuttered in a large binder.

### A restored backup is written away from the main thread
- **Decided:** the importer runs on a context made for it in a background task. `UnitOfWork.performPrivate` is the unit for such a context: one save at the end, everything rolled back on failure. It needs no bookkeeping because no one else has the context. Actions that save through `UnitOfWork.save` stay on the main actor's units. The main-thread import and the background one share one write path.
- **Why:** writing a large backup paused the screen.

### The share inbox has a version, and nothing in it is skipped silently
- **Decided:** each inbox manifest carries a version; one without reads as the first, a newer one is set aside. An item that can't be opened is moved aside once and shown as *Nu am putut deschide* with *Șterge*, never skipped on every look. The practice invoice is known by the fingerprint the app leaves before showing the share sheet (valid one hour), not by its file name; the name check is kept, unused, as the way back.
- **Why:** an item the app couldn't read stayed invisible in the inbox forever, and a practice file renamed on the way became a real document.

### Motion and the delayed spinner are shared with the extensions
- **Decided:** `DS.Motion`, `dsAnimation` and `DSDelayedProgress` live in `ios/Shared`, so the share sheet uses the app's springs and waits the same time before a spinner. A steady sweep (the skeleton's shimmer) and the short confirmation dwell are tokens too.
- **Why:** the share sheet had its own spring, ease-out curve, spinner delay and auto-close.

### First-version code nothing uses is deleted, not moved
- **Decided:** the day-count cadence heuristic, the `.annual` interval, the calculator's own day difference, the cover, bonus-malus and component catalogs, and the PDF parse and thumbnail helpers are deleted. Nothing referenced them, so nothing is being replaced; the history keeps them. `ios/Legacy/` is unchanged.
- **Open for the owner:** the agreed fix list said "move to Legacy, ask before deleting". If you want them kept there, they come back from the commit in one step.
- **Why:** a second copy nobody uses is the one a later change picks by mistake.

### A passport is good for travel until six months before it expires
- **Decided (owner):** only passports have a travel cut-off: six months before the printed expiry. Many countries refuse entry with less left, and 6 months covers every country's rule, including those that ask for 3. It is fixed: no per-country option, no setting. ID cards are valid for travel until they expire; driving licences, certificates and every other document keep their plain expiry, with no "bun de călătorit" line, no earlier reminders and no earlier urgency.
- **Decided:** it is data on the document type, not a passport case in code: `DocumentType.travelMargins` gives the passport type (and so every variant of it) 180 days, as whole calendar months; no other type has one and there is no default. The catalog's passport profile takes it from there (`ReminderProfile.travelMarginDays`), the deadline keeps it (database version 4, `Obligation.travelMarginDays`) and the one urgency rule carries it (`UrgencyRule.travelMarginDays`, in the widget snapshot too).
- **Decided:** what the cut-off changes, all from that one number:
  - the page and the document's hero show both dates, "Expiră pe …" and "Bun de călătorit până pe …" ("A fost bun de călătorit până pe …" once it has passed);
